# MC Server Manager — Plan 4: Backups (includes jobs)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Native backups in the agent —manual and scheduled by cron, `tar.zst` with `save-off`/`save-all flush`/`save-on` guaranteed, pruning by days and count, restore that never deletes the previous data— and a generic job system in the panel with progress and live log, Backups and History tabs, and a Jobs page.

**Architecture:** The agent adds the `backup` package (tar.zst archive with exclusions and zip-slip protection, names/metadata/pruning, create and restore, cron scheduler with injectable clock), the `jobs` package (job registry in bbolt, idempotent by `job_id`, which emits `JobProgress`) and a per-server reservation in the reconciler (`Hold`) so that an `ApplySpec`, a `PowerAction` or a `RemoveServer` does not touch the container in the middle of a backup or restore. The panel adds the `jobs` and `backups` tables, a generic `JobService` (topic `jobs` of the browser WS; half-done jobs → `failed` "interrupted" at startup, except the agent's jobs) and a `BackupService` that gives orders to the agent and reconciles its inventory after each `Hello`. The web adds the Backups tab (list, create, restore, delete, schedule), the History tab and the Jobs page.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27.2, `github.com/klauspost/compress/zstd` v1.20.1, `github.com/robfig/cron/v3` v3.0.1 (parser only), bbolt; Node 26, Fastify 5, `node:sqlite`, zod 4, `@bufbuild/protobuf` 2.16; React 19, TanStack Query/Router, shadcn/ui; buf 1.73.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mc-server-manager-design.md` (§4 `backups`/`jobs`, §4.1 `backups`, §5.3–5.4, §6.5, §7 `JobService`/`BackupService`, §8 Backups and History tabs and Jobs page, §10). Scope and acceptance: `docs/superpowers/plans/README.md` (Plan 4). Wave A rules: `docs/superpowers/plans/COORDINATION.md`.

**Starting point and prior verification.** This plan was written against the code of Plan 1 of 2026-10-12 (agent `feat/plan-1-agent` e1158bb, panel `feat/plan-1` 799232c, web `feat/plan-1-web` + brief of its Task 18). All its code —Go, TS and TSX— was compiled and tested on a copy of that code before being written here: `buf lint`/`buf breaking`, `go vet` and `go test -race ./...`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test` (shared 73, api 83, web 64 tests) and `pnpm --filter @mcsm/web build`, and the integration test of Task 16 passed against real Docker (itzg VANILLA, 57 s). If when running it a Plan 1 name has changed after integration (e.g. the variables `s`/`node` in `server.tsx`), use the real one and note it.

The anchors already exist (preparation commit of the wave); if any is missing, stop and tell the controller: do not create it in another position.

Atomic writes (`.partial` + rename). A valid backup name never contains `/` or `..`.

- New dependencies: only the two Go ones cited. API verified with `go doc`: `zstd.NewWriter(w, zstd.WithEncoderLevel(zstd.SpeedDefault)) (*Encoder, error)`, `(*Encoder).Close() error`, `zstd.NewReader(r) (*Decoder, error)`, `(*Decoder).Close()` (no error); `cron.ParseStandard(expr) (cron.Schedule, error)`, `Schedule.Next(time.Time) time.Time`. The robfig runner is not used (real clock, not injectable). No new npm dependency.
- Plan 4 does **not** implement backup download (the HTTP transfers of Plan 5): the "Download" button stays disabled. It does **not** touch `auth/**`, the session guard or the configuration form of Plan 3: the schedule is edited in the Backups tab with `PATCH /api/servers/:id/backups/schedule`.
- UI and error messages in Spanish; identifiers in English. HTTP errors `{ error }`; 409 if the node is disconnected or the server busy; 502 with the agent's text if the agent rejects the order.
- Tests without `time.Sleep`/`setTimeout` to synchronize: injectable clocks and schedulers, channels and events; a `time.After` only as a safety net of a `select`. `-race` clean.
- Docker on the development machine: only resources `mcsm-test-*`, always cleaned up.
- The time of the cron expressions is the node's (`TZ` variable of the agent container; the agent image ships `tzdata`; without `TZ`, UTC).
- Commits: conventional commits in Spanish; the last `Co-Authored-By:` line is that of the model implementing the task (Sonnet 5.5 by default; Opus 5.5 in those of `systems-expert`; Haiku 5.5 in those of `mechanical-dev`).

## Review Focus

1. **`save-on` guaranteed.** If the backup fails, is cancelled (the agent shuts down) or the `save-off` reaches the server but its response does not, the server must end with autosave enabled; a world with a permanent `save-off` loses everything played since then. The `defer` of `save-on` is registered **before** the `save-off` and uses its own context (`context.WithoutCancel` + 15 s). Tests: Task 6 (cancellation after the flush, `save-off` failure, failure in the middle of the backup, failure of `save-on` itself which keeps the backup and warns), Task 9 (RCON deadline of 2 min for `save-all flush` on large worlds) and Task 16 (real Docker: after the backup, `save-on` answers "already").
2. **Restore never deletes the previous directory and a failure leaves the data intact.** It extracts into `data.restoring-<ts>`, verifies the sha256 beforehand, and only at the end swaps the directories; the previous data stays in `data.pre-restore-<ts>` and is recorded as a `pre_restore` backup that is only deleted by hand. Tests: Task 6 (damaged or truncated backup → `data` intact and no temporaries; two restores → two pre-restores), Task 9 (pre-restore announced and server started again), Task 14 (the UI only offers Delete, with confirmation).
3. **Zip-slip and paths.** A `.tar.zst` with `../`, absolute paths or a symbolic link followed by a file "inside" the link cannot write outside the destination; a backup name with `../` cannot delete or restore anything outside `backups/`. Tests: Task 4 (`../evil`, `a/../../evil`, absolute, symlink+file, hardlink), Task 5 (`ValidName`, `Delete` with hostile names), Task 9 (`BackupRestore`/`BackupDelete` with `../`).
4. **Concurrent backup with an `ApplySpec` (or power/remove).** With the server reserved (`Hold`), a spec change is saved but does not recreate the container until the backup ends; power and remove return "busy"; a second backup or a simultaneous restore is rejected. Tests: Task 8 (reservation, release that applies the deferred change, waiting for the running operation) and Task 9 (`ApplySpec` while `save-all flush` is blocked: zero recreations; when it ends, v2 applied).
5. **Idempotency by `job_id` and restarts.** A repeated `BackupNow` does not make two backups (it resends the saved result); a restart of the agent marks its half-done jobs as failed; a restart of the panel marks open jobs as `failed` "interrupted" except `backup`/`restore`, which are resolved with the agent's inventory on reconnection (those the agent does not know → failed; those created after the inventory was requested are not touched). Tests: Task 3, Task 9, Task 10 (two `buildApp` over the same file), Task 11.
6. **Pruning by `keepDays`/`keepCount`.** Only scheduled backups count, never the most recent one is deleted, 0 = no limit; manual and pre-restore backups are not pruned. With `* * * * *` and `keepCount` N, N files remain. Tests: Task 5 (`PrunePlan` table), Task 9 (3 runs with `keepCount` 2 → 2 backups + one `BackupDeleted`; a manual one does not count) and Task 16 (acceptance with the real agent).

## Divergences from the spec (decided here)

1. `BackupRestore` carries `filename` instead of `backup_id`: the agent does not know the panel's ids and the name is unique per server.
2. `BackupList` is answered with `Ack` followed by an unsolicited `BackupInventory` (`id` 0) that also carries the jobs: this way there is no need to add cases to `RESPONSE_CASES` in `agents/hub.ts`, which has no anchor.
3. Restore extracts first into `data.restoring-<ts>` and then swaps directories (the spec renames and then extracts). Same final result; an extraction failure does not leave the server without data.
4. Pruning only considers `scheduled` backups and never deletes the most recent one (the spec does not say so; deleting manual backups by retention would be a surprise).
5. Default exclusions `logs`, `cache`, `*.tmp`, without `*.jar` (the spec example included it): `.jar` files uploaded by hand (Plan 5) cannot be downloaded again. The UI explains how to add it.
6. Cron: common syntax of 5 numeric fields (`*`, `,`, `-`, `/`), without names, `?` or `@daily`, validated the same way in Go and in zod with a shared fixture; the scheduler uses `cron.ParseStandard` and its own loop with injectable clock.
7. The rows of `backups` are created when the agent reports a finished backup (`ok`) and become `deleted` when deleted; a failed backup remains only as a `failed` job with its cause (the partial one is deleted). The states `running`/`failed` are kept in the `CHECK` for compatibility with the spec.
8. The `restore` jobs, like the `backup` ones, survive a restart of the panel: both are run by the agent and are resolved with its inventory.
9. The History tab shows jobs; the "events" of the spec will arrive when the `Event` message exists (it is not in the protocol).
10. `onlyIfPlayersSince` is decided with the players seen in each observed state (every ~10 s, in memory); if the agent restarted after the last backup, the backup is made (when in doubt, back up).

## Coordination with plans 2, 3 and 5 (and wave B)

- **Foreseeable merge conflicts outside anchors (independent insertions: both are kept):** `panel/apps/api/src/realtime/hub.ts` (Plan 2 inserts its `node_metrics` listener at the same point of the constructor; Plan 4 also widens the `TOPIC` regex with `jobs`), `panel/apps/web/src/lib/ws.ts` (`topicOf`: Plan 2 rewrites the `case "node"` line in front of the one Plan 4 inserts its own into) and `lib/api.ts` (`ServerMessage`) with Plan 2; `components/layout.tsx` and `router.tsx` with Plan 3 ("Templates"); `panel/packages/shared/src/index.ts` with plans 2 and 3.
- **`agent/internal/mc/mc.go` with Plan 2 (safe conflict, not mechanical):** Plan 2 moves the body of `Rcon` to `DialRcon`/`RconConn.Command` keeping the 5 s cap. When merging, the rule of Task 9 —if the context carries a deadline, the context wins (`rconDeadline`)— must be applied where Plan 2 computes the deadline; `TestRconDeadline` protects it. Without it, a `save-all flush` longer than 5 s on a large world makes the backup fail (with `save-on` sent anyway).
- **Plan 3:** turns `PATCH /api/servers/:id` into a first-level merge (absent key = kept) and foresees that the Backups tab sends `{ spec: { backups } }`. Plan 4 uses its own route `PATCH /api/servers/:id/backups/schedule` (it does not depend on that merge) and adds in `completeSpec` the line that keeps `backups` with the current `PATCH`; after Plan 3's merge that line is redundant but harmless. If it clashes, keep Plan 3's version and check that the test "…the configuration form does not delete it" (Task 12) stays green. Plan 3 also configures zod in Spanish (`z.locales.es()`), which translates the type errors of this plan's 400s.
- **Plan 5:** changes `RESPONSE_CASES` in `agents/hub.ts` (Plan 4 does not touch it) and provides `app.transfers`, which the download of backups in wave B will use.
- **Plan 5 / wave B (download):** "Download" needs `BackupPush{server_id, filename, transfer_id}` (agent → panel via `PUT /agent/transfer/:id`) and a route `GET /api/servers/:id/backups/:backupId/download` that streams to the browser; Plan 4 leaves the button disabled and fields 58–69 free for it.
- **Plan 6 (import, `dataPath`):** the agent takes the data directory from `spec.BuildContainer(...).DataDir`, so an explicit `dataPath` is respected without changes; the pre-restore directory is created next to it.
- **Plan 7 (migration):** reuses `BackupNow{trigger: PRE_MIGRATION}`, `BackupRestore` by `filename`, the jobs and `Hold`; it will add `BackupPull`.
- **Generated code and lockfiles:** `agent/gen/**`, `panel/packages/proto-ts/src/gen/**`, `agent/go.sum` and `pnpm-lock.yaml` are regenerated after merging (`pnpm gen`, `go mod tidy`, `pnpm install`).

### Task 1: Backup and job protocol

**Agent:** mechanical-dev (the brief brings the complete code).

**Files:**
- Modify: `proto/mcsm/v1/agent.proto` (oneof `body` and message zone, below the `plan-4` anchors)
- Regenerate: `agent/gen/mcsm/v1/agent.pb.go`, `panel/packages/proto-ts/src/gen/mcsm/v1/agent_pb.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Envelope`, `Ack`, `Error` from Plan 1.
- Produces (Go `mcsmv1`, TS `@mcsm/proto`): enums `BackupTrigger` (`MANUAL`, `SCHEDULED`, `PRE_MIGRATION`, `PRE_RESTORE`) and `JobStatus` (`RUNNING`, `OK`, `FAILED`); messages `BackupInfo`, `BackupNow`, `BackupRestore`, `BackupDelete`, `BackupList`, `BackupInventory`, `BackupCreated`, `BackupDeleted`, `JobProgress`, `JobRecord`; oneof cases `backupNow` (50), `backupRestore` (51), `backupDelete` (52), `backupList` (53), `backupInventory` (54), `backupCreated` (55), `backupDeleted` (56), `jobProgress` (57). In Go: `mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupNow{BackupNow: …}`, `mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK`, `mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_SCHEDULED`… In TS: `{ case: "backupNow", value: … }`, `JobStatus.OK`, `BackupTrigger.MANUAL`; the `uint64`/`int64` are `bigint`.
- Exchanges: panel → agent `BackupNow`, `BackupRestore`, `BackupDelete` (answer `Ack`/`Error` with `code` `busy` | `unknown_server` | `invalid_request` | `backup_failed`), `BackupList` (answer `Ack` + `BackupInventory` with `id` 0). Agent → panel with `id` 0: `JobProgress`, `BackupCreated`, `BackupDeleted`, `BackupInventory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Messages**

At the end of the file, below the `// ── plan-4 ──` anchor of the message zone:
```proto
// Backups (spec §6.5) and long jobs of the agent (spec §5.3–5.4).

enum BackupTrigger {
  BACKUP_TRIGGER_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  BACKUP_TRIGGER_MANUAL = 1;
  BACKUP_TRIGGER_SCHEDULED = 2;
  BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_MIGRATION = 3;
  BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_RESTORE = 4;
}

enum JobStatus {
  JOB_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  JOB_STATUS_RUNNING = 1;
  JOB_STATUS_OK = 2;
  JOB_STATUS_FAILED = 3;
}

// BackupInfo describes a backup on the node's disk. `filename` is the .tar.zst in
// <DATA_ROOT>/<slug>/backups/ or, with trigger PRE_RESTORE, the directory
// data.pre-restore-<ts> that is kept next to data/.
message BackupInfo {
  string server_id = 1;
  string filename = 2;
  uint64 size_bytes = 3;
  string sha256 = 4; // empty in the pre-restore directories
  uint64 spec_version = 5;
  BackupTrigger trigger = 6;
  int64 started_unix_ms = 7;
  int64 finished_unix_ms = 8;
}

// Panel → agent. Answer: Ack (accepted; progress arrives via JobProgress) or Error
// (code busy | unknown_server | invalid_request | backup_failed). A repeated job_id
// is not run twice: the agent resends its saved state.
message BackupNow {
  string server_id = 1;
  string job_id = 2;
  BackupTrigger trigger = 3; // MANUAL if not given; PRE_MIGRATION will be used by Plan 7
}

// Panel → agent. Restores a .tar.zst backup by name (the agent does not know the
// panel's ids). Answer: Ack or Error, as BackupNow.
message BackupRestore {
  string server_id = 1;
  string filename = 2;
  string job_id = 3;
}

// Panel → agent. Deletes a backup or a pre-restore directory. Answer: Ack or Error.
message BackupDelete {
  string server_id = 1;
  string filename = 2;
}

// Panel → agent. Answer: Ack and then an unsolicited BackupInventory (id 0).
// server_id empty = all the servers of the node.
message BackupList {
  string server_id = 1;
}

// Agent → panel (id 0). `server_ids` are the servers whose listing is complete: the
// panel marks as deleted the backups of those servers that do not appear in `backups`.
message BackupInventory {
  repeated string server_ids = 1;
  repeated BackupInfo backups = 2;
  repeated JobRecord jobs = 3;
}

// Agent → panel (id 0): new backup (manual, scheduled or pre-restore data).
message BackupCreated {
  BackupInfo backup = 1;
  string job_id = 2; // empty for the pre-restore ones
}

// Agent → panel (id 0): backup deleted by the agent itself (pruning).
message BackupDeleted {
  string server_id = 1;
  string filename = 2;
  string reason = 3;
}

// Agent → panel (id 0): progress of a job. log_chunk carries only the new lines.
message JobProgress {
  string job_id = 1;
  string server_id = 2;
  string type = 3; // backup | restore
  JobStatus status = 4;
  uint32 progress = 5; // 0-100
  string message = 6;
  string log_chunk = 7;
  int64 ts_unix_ms = 8;
}

// Full state of a job saved in the agent (inventory after reconnecting).
message JobRecord {
  string job_id = 1;
  string server_id = 2;
  string type = 3;
  JobStatus status = 4;
  uint32 progress = 5;
  string message = 6;
  string log = 7;
  int64 started_unix_ms = 8;
  int64 finished_unix_ms = 9;
}
```

**Agent:** go-agent-dev (includes the twin zod schema).

**Files:**
- Create: `proto/fixtures/cron-cases.json`, `agent/internal/spec/backups.go`, `agent/internal/spec/backups_test.go`, `panel/packages/shared/src/backups.ts`, `panel/packages/shared/src/backups.test.ts`
- Modify: `agent/internal/spec/spec.go` (`plan-4` anchors of the `Spec` struct and of `Validate`), `panel/packages/shared/src/spec.ts` (import + `plan-4` anchor of `baseFields`), `panel/packages/shared/src/index.ts`, `agent/go.mod`/`go.sum`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `spec.Spec`, `spec.Parse`, `spec.BuildContainer` (Plan 1); `specSchema`/`specInputSchema` (Plan 1).
- Produces (Go): `type spec.Backups struct{ Enabled bool; Cron string; KeepDays, KeepCount int; Excludes []string; OnlyIfPlayersSince bool }` (JSON `enabled`, `cron`, `keepDays`, `keepCount`, `excludes`, `onlyIfPlayersSince`), field `Spec.Backups *Backups` (`json:"backups,omitempty"`), `func spec.ValidCron(expr string) error`, `func (*Backups) Validate() error`, `func (Spec) BackupExcludes() []string`, `var spec.DefaultBackupExcludes = []string{"logs", "cache", "*.tmp"}`.
- Produces (TS, `@mcsm/shared`): `backupsSchema` (defaults `enabled:false`, `cron:"0 4 * * *"`, `keepDays:7`, `keepCount:10`, `excludes:["logs","cache","*.tmp"]`, `onlyIfPlayersSince:true`), `type BackupsConfig`, `type BackupsInput`, `cronError(expr): string | null`, `DEFAULT_BACKUP_EXCLUDES`; `specSchema`/`specInputSchema` accept an optional `backups`.
- The section is **optional**: without it there are no scheduled backups. Changing it does not change the container hash (the container is not recreated).

`agent/internal/spec/backups_test.go`:
```go
package spec

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestValidCronFixture(t *testing.T) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join("..", "..", "..", "proto", "fixtures", "cron-cases.json"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var cases struct{ Valid, Invalid []string }
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &cases); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(cases.Valid) == 0 || len(cases.Invalid) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("fixture empty")
	}
	for _, e := range cases.Valid {
		if err := ValidCron(e); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%q should be valid: %v", e, err)
		}
	}
	for _, e := range cases.Invalid {
		if err := ValidCron(e); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%q should be invalid", e)
		}
	}
}

func TestBackupsValidate(t *testing.T) {
	ok := &Backups{Enabled: true, Cron: "0 4 * * *", KeepDays: 7, KeepCount: 10, Excludes: []string{"logs", "*.tmp", "world/session.lock"}, OnlyIfPlayersSince: true}
	if err := ok.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := (*Backups)(nil).Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := (&Backups{}).Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("disabled and without cron must be valid: %v", err)
	}
	bad := []Backups{
		{Enabled: true, Cron: ""},
		{Enabled: true, Cron: "0 4 * * MON"},
		{Cron: "0 4 * *"},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", KeepDays: -1},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", KeepCount: MaxBackupKeepCount + 1},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", Excludes: []string{"/etc"}},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", Excludes: []string{"../world"}},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", Excludes: []string{"[abc]"}},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", Excludes: []string{`a\b`}},
		{Cron: "0 4 * * *", Excludes: []string{""}},
	}
	for i, b := range bad {
		if err := b.Validate(); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("case %d %+v should fail", i, b)
		}
	}
}

func TestSpecWithBackups(t *testing.T) {
	s := loadFixture(t)
	if s.Backups != nil {
		t.Fatal("the fixture does not carry backups")
	}
	if got := strings.Join(s.BackupExcludes(), ","); got != "logs,cache,*.tmp" {
		t.Fatalf("default excludes = %s", got)
	}
	before, err := BuildContainer(meta, s, "/srv/minecraft", "mcsm")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.Backups = &Backups{Enabled: true, Cron: "*/5 * * * *", KeepCount: 3, Excludes: []string{}}
	if err := s.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(s.BackupExcludes()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("an explicit empty excludes list is respected")
	}
	after, err := BuildContainer(meta, s, "/srv/minecraft", "mcsm")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if before.Hash != after.Hash {
		t.Fatal("changing the backup schedule must not recreate the container")
	}
	s.Backups.Cron = "0 4 * * SUN"
	if err := s.Validate(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "backups.cron") {
		t.Fatalf("Validate must validate backups: %v", err)
	}
	raw := []byte(`{"type":"PAPER","version":"1.21.4","memory":"2G","ports":{"game":25565},"rconPassword":"abcdefgh",
		"backups":{"enabled":true,"cron":"0 4 * * *","keepDays":7,"keepCount":10,"excludes":["logs"],"onlyIfPlayersSince":true}}`)
	p, err := Parse(raw)
	if err != nil || p.Backups == nil || !p.Backups.OnlyIfPlayersSince || p.Backups.KeepCount != 10 || p.Backups.Cron != "0 4 * * *" {
		t.Fatalf("parse = %+v, %v", p.Backups, err)
	}
}
```

`agent/internal/spec/backups.go`:
```go
package spec

import (
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/robfig/cron/v3"
)

// Backups is the schedule and retention of a server's backups (spec §4.1). If the spec
// does not carry it (nil) there are no scheduled backups and manual ones use DefaultBackupExcludes.
type Backups struct {
	Enabled            bool     `json:"enabled"`
	Cron               string   `json:"cron"`
	KeepDays           int      `json:"keepDays"`
	KeepCount          int      `json:"keepCount"`
	Excludes           []string `json:"excludes"`
	OnlyIfPlayersSince bool     `json:"onlyIfPlayersSince"`
}

// DefaultBackupExcludes does not include `*.jar`: the .jar files uploaded by hand (mods,
// plugins) cannot be downloaded again, so by default they go into the backup.
var DefaultBackupExcludes = []string{"logs", "cache", "*.tmp"}

const (
	MaxBackupKeepDays  = 3650
	MaxBackupKeepCount = 1000
	MaxBackupExcludes  = 50
	maxCronLen         = 100
)

// excludeRe: relative patterns with letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `-`, `*`, `?` and `/` (not at
// the start). No brackets or backslashes: path.Match never returns ErrBadPattern and the
// panel validates exactly the same with zod.
var excludeRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9._*?-][A-Za-z0-9._*?/-]{0,199}$`)

var cronItemRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(\*|\d{1,2}|\d{1,2}-\d{1,2})(/\d{1,2})?$`)

var cronFields = [5]struct {
	name     string
	min, max int
}{{"minute", 0, 59}, {"hour", 0, 23}, {"day of month", 1, 31}, {"month", 1, 12}, {"day of week", 0, 6}}

// ValidCron accepts the common 5-field syntax (minute hour day month day-of-week) with
// numbers, `*`, `,`, `-` and `/`. No names (MON, JAN), no `?`, no descriptors (@daily) and
// no CRON_TZ: the panel validates the same (fixture proto/fixtures/cron-cases.json).
func ValidCron(expr string) error {
	if len(expr) > maxCronLen {
		return fmt.Errorf("cron expression too long")
	}
	fields := strings.Fields(expr)
	if len(fields) != 5 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid cron expression %q: it must have 5 fields (minute hour day month day-of-week)", expr)
	}
	for i, f := range fields {
		lim := cronFields[i]
		for _, item := range strings.Split(f, ",") {
			m := cronItemRe.FindStringSubmatch(item)
			if m == nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("invalid cron expression %q: %s %q not valid", expr, lim.name, f)
			}
			if m[1] != "*" {
				lo, hi, isRange := strings.Cut(m[1], "-")
				if !isRange {
					hi = lo
				}
				l, _ := strconv.Atoi(lo)
				h, _ := strconv.Atoi(hi)
				if l < lim.min || h > lim.max || l > h {
					return fmt.Errorf("invalid cron expression %q: %s out of range (%d-%d)", expr, lim.name, lim.min, lim.max)
				}
			}
			if m[2] != "" {
				if step, _ := strconv.Atoi(m[2][1:]); step < 1 {
					return fmt.Errorf("invalid cron expression %q: the step of %s must be at least 1", expr, lim.name)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if _, err := cron.ParseStandard(expr); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid cron expression %q: %w", expr, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// Validate checks the backups section (nil is valid: no schedule).
func (b *Backups) Validate() error {
	if b == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if b.Enabled || b.Cron != "" {
		if err := ValidCron(b.Cron); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("spec: backups.cron: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if b.KeepDays < 0 || b.KeepDays > MaxBackupKeepDays {
		return fmt.Errorf("spec: backups.keepDays out of range (0-%d)", MaxBackupKeepDays)
	}
	if b.KeepCount < 0 || b.KeepCount > MaxBackupKeepCount {
		return fmt.Errorf("spec: backups.keepCount out of range (0-%d)", MaxBackupKeepCount)
	}
	if len(b.Excludes) > MaxBackupExcludes {
		return fmt.Errorf("spec: backups.excludes allows at most %d patterns", MaxBackupExcludes)
	}
	for _, p := range b.Excludes {
		if !excludeRe.MatchString(p) || strings.Contains(p, "..") {
			return fmt.Errorf("spec: backups.excludes: invalid pattern %q", p)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// BackupExcludes returns the effective excludes (a copy: the caller may modify it).
func (s Spec) BackupExcludes() []string {
	if s.Backups == nil {
		return append([]string(nil), DefaultBackupExcludes...)
	}
	return append([]string(nil), s.Backups.Excludes...)
}
```

`panel/packages/shared/src/backups.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/** Same default excludes as the agent (`spec.DefaultBackupExcludes`). */
export const DEFAULT_BACKUP_EXCLUDES: readonly string[] = ["logs", "cache", "*.tmp"];

const EXCLUDE_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9._*?-][A-Za-z0-9._*?/-]{0,199}$/;
const CRON_ITEM = /^(\*|\d{1,2}|\d{1,2}-\d{1,2})(\/\d{1,2})?$/;
const CRON_FIELDS: readonly (readonly [string, number, number])[] = [
  ["Minute", 0, 59], ["Hour", 0, 23], ["Day of month", 1, 31], ["Month", 1, 12], ["Day of week", 0, 6],
];

/** Same criteria as `spec.ValidCron` in the agent. Returns the error message or null if it is valid. */
export function cronError(expr: string): string | null {
  if (expr.length > 100) return "Cron expression too long";
  const fields = expr.trim().split(/\s+/).filter((f) => f !== "");
  if (fields.length !== 5) return "The cron expression must have 5 fields: minute hour day month day-of-week";
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const [name, min, max] = CRON_FIELDS[i]!;
    const field = fields[i]!;
    for (const item of field.split(",")) {
      const m = CRON_ITEM.exec(item);
      if (!m) return `Field "${name}" not valid: ${field}`;
      if (m[1] !== "*") {
        const [lo, hi = lo] = m[1]!.split("-");
        const l = Number(lo);
        const h = Number(hi);
        if (l < min || h > max || l > h) return `${name} out of range (${min}-${max})`;
      }
      if (m[2] && Number(m[2].slice(1)) < 1) return `The step of "${name}" must be at least 1`;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const excludePattern = z
  .string()
  .regex(EXCLUDE_RE, "Invalid exclude pattern (no leading /, no brackets; e.g. logs, *.tmp, world/session.lock)")
  .refine((p) => !p.includes(".."), { message: "An exclude pattern cannot contain .." });

/** Schedule and retention of backups (spec §4.1 `backups`). Same as `spec.Backups` in the agent. */
export const backupsSchema = z
  .object({
    enabled: z.boolean().default(false),
    cron: z.string().max(100).default("0 4 * * *"),
    keepDays: z.number().int().min(0).max(3650).default(7),
    keepCount: z.number().int().min(0).max(1000).default(10),
    excludes: z.array(excludePattern).max(50).default(() => [...DEFAULT_BACKUP_EXCLUDES]),
    onlyIfPlayersSince: z.boolean().default(true),
  })
  .superRefine((b, ctx) => {
    if (!b.enabled && b.cron === "") return;
    const err = cronError(b.cron);
    if (err) ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["cron"], message: err });
  });

export type BackupsConfig = z.infer<typeof backupsSchema>;
export type BackupsInput = z.input<typeof backupsSchema>;
```

**Agent:** go-agent-dev.

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/store/jobs.go`, `agent/internal/store/jobs_test.go`, `agent/internal/jobs/jobs.go`, `agent/internal/jobs/jobs_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `*store.Store` and its test helper `open(t)` (Plan 1); `mcsmv1.JobRecord`, `mcsmv1.JobProgress`, `mcsmv1.JobStatus` (Task 1).
- Produces (store): `(*Store).PutJob(*mcsmv1.JobRecord) error`, `GetJob(id) (*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)` (nil, nil if it does not exist), `ListJobs() ([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)`, `PruneJobs(before time.Time) (int, error)`. The bucket `jobs` is created on first use (`Open` is not touched).
- Produces (jobs): `jobs.TypeBackup = "backup"`, `jobs.TypeRestore = "restore"`, `jobs.MaxLog`, `jobs.KeepFor`; `jobs.New(st jobs.Store, send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error, now func() time.Time) *Tracker`; `jobs.NewID() string` (UUID v4); `(*Tracker).Begin(id, serverID, typ string) (*Job, *mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)` — with an already known id it returns `(nil, savedRecord, nil)` and the job **must not** run again; `(*Tracker).Replay(*mcsmv1.JobRecord)`; `(*Tracker).FailInterrupted() error`; `(*Tracker).Inventory(serverID string) ([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)` (7 days, max 200, log trimmed to 4 KiB); `(*Job).ID() string`, `Progress(pct uint32, msg string)`, `Log(line string)`, `Done(err error, okMsg string)`, `Record() *mcsmv1.JobRecord`.
- Emission: `Begin` emits RUNNING; `Progress` only if the message changes or it rises ≥ 5 points (it never goes back, cap 99); `Log` emits `log_chunk`; `Done` emits OK (100) or FAILED with the error. The texts are sanitized to valid UTF-8 (proto3 rejects invalid strings).

- [ ] **Step 2: Store implementation**

`agent/internal/store/jobs.go`:
```go
package store

import (
	"errors"
	"time"

	"go.etcd.io/bbolt"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
)

// bJobs stores the long jobs (backup, restore) by job_id: this way the agent answers a
// repeated job_id with its result (spec §5.4) and reports to the panel after reconnecting.
// The bucket is created on first use so as not to touch the bucket list of Open.
var bJobs = []byte("jobs")

// PutJob saves (or replaces) the record of a job.
func (s *Store) PutJob(j *mcsmv1.JobRecord) error {
	if j.GetJobId() == "" {
		return errors.New("store: job without id")
	}
	raw, err := proto.Marshal(j)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b, err := tx.CreateBucketIfNotExists(bJobs)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return b.Put([]byte(j.JobId), raw)
	})
}

// GetJob returns the job or nil if it does not exist.
func (s *Store) GetJob(id string) (*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error) {
	var out *mcsmv1.JobRecord
	err := s.db.View(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b := tx.Bucket(bJobs)
		if b == nil {
			return nil
		}
		raw := b.Get([]byte(id))
		if raw == nil {
			return nil
		}
		j := &mcsmv1.JobRecord{}
		if err := proto.Unmarshal(raw, j); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		out = j
		return nil
	})
	return out, err
}

// ListJobs returns all the saved jobs.
func (s *Store) ListJobs() ([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error) {
	var out []*mcsmv1.JobRecord
	err := s.db.View(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b := tx.Bucket(bJobs)
		if b == nil {
			return nil
		}
		return b.ForEach(func(_, raw []byte) error {
			j := &mcsmv1.JobRecord{}
			if err := proto.Unmarshal(raw, j); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			out = append(out, j)
			return nil
		})
	})
	return out, err
}

// PruneJobs deletes the jobs finished before `before` (never the RUNNING ones). Returns how many.
func (s *Store) PruneJobs(before time.Time) (int, error) {
	n := 0
	err := s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b := tx.Bucket(bJobs)
		if b == nil {
			return nil
		}
		var del [][]byte
		err := b.ForEach(func(k, raw []byte) error {
			j := &mcsmv1.JobRecord{}
			if err := proto.Unmarshal(raw, j); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if j.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING && j.FinishedUnixMs > 0 && j.FinishedUnixMs < before.UnixMilli() {
				del = append(del, append([]byte(nil), k...)) // k is only valid inside the transaction
			}
			return nil
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, k := range del {
			if err := b.Delete(k); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		n = len(del)
		return nil
	})
	return n, err
}
```

```go
	"time"
	"unicode/utf8"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/store"
)

type progressSink struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	msgs []*mcsmv1.JobProgress
}

func (s *progressSink) send(e *mcsmv1.Envelope) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if p := e.GetJobProgress(); p != nil {
		s.msgs = append(s.msgs, p)
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *progressSink) all() []*mcsmv1.JobProgress {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]*mcsmv1.JobProgress(nil), s.msgs...)
}

type testClock struct{ t time.Time }

func (c *testClock) now() time.Time { return c.t }

func setup(t *testing.T) (*Tracker, *store.Store, *progressSink, *testClock) {
	t.Helper()
	st, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { st.Close() })
	s := &progressSink{}
	c := &testClock{t: time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
	return New(st, s.send, c.now), st, s, c
}

const (
	running = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING
	ok      = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK
	failed  = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_FAILED
)

func TestJobLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	tr, st, s, _ := setup(t)
	j, old, err := tr.Begin("j1", "srv", TypeBackup)
	if err != nil || old != nil || j == nil {
		t.Fatalf("Begin = %v, %v, %v", j, old, err)
	}
	j.Progress(3, "Copying data") // message changes: emitted
	j.Progress(4, "Copying data") // +1: not emitted
	j.Progress(9, "Copying data") // ≥ 3+5: emitted
	j.Progress(2, "Copying data") // does not go back, not emitted
	j.Log("save-off")
	j.Done(nil, "Backup created")
	j.Done(errors.New("late"), "") // ignored
	msgs := s.all()
	if len(msgs) != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("emitted %d: %+v", len(msgs), msgs)
	}
	if msgs[0].Status != running || msgs[0].Type != TypeBackup || msgs[0].ServerId != "srv" {
		t.Fatalf("first notice = %+v", msgs[0])
	}
	if msgs[2].Progress != 9 || msgs[3].LogChunk != "save-off\n" {
		t.Fatalf("notices = %+v", msgs)
	}
	if last := msgs[4]; last.Status != ok || last.Progress != 100 || last.Message != "Backup created" {
		t.Fatalf("final = %+v", last)
	}
	rec, _ := st.GetJob("j1")
	if rec.Status != ok || rec.Log != "save-off\n" || rec.FinishedUnixMs == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("saved = %+v", rec)
	}
}

func TestDuplicateJobIsNotRunTwice(t *testing.T) {
	tr, _, s, _ := setup(t)
	j, _, _ := tr.Begin("j1", "srv", TypeBackup)
	j.Done(nil, "ok")
	j2, old, err := tr.Begin("j1", "srv", TypeBackup)
	if err != nil || j2 != nil || old == nil || old.Status != ok {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate Begin = %v, %+v, %v", j2, old, err)
	}
	n := len(s.all())
	tr.Replay(old)
	got := s.all()
	if len(got) != n+1 || got[n].Status != ok || got[n].JobId != "j1" {
		t.Fatalf("Replay = %+v", got[n:])
	}
}

func TestDoneWithErrorAndInvalidUTF8(t *testing.T) {
	tr, st, _, _ := setup(t)
	j, _, _ := tr.Begin("j1", "srv", TypeRestore)
	j.Log("odd file: \xff\xfe")
	j.Done(errors.New("the backup is damaged \xff"), "")
	rec, err := st.GetJob("j1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if rec.Status != failed || !strings.HasPrefix(rec.Message, "the backup is damaged") {
		t.Fatalf("rec = %+v", rec)
	}
	if !utf8.ValidString(rec.Log) || !utf8.ValidString(rec.Message) {
		t.Fatal("log and message must be valid UTF-8")
	}
}

func TestFailInterrupted(t *testing.T) {
	tr, st, _, _ := setup(t)
	tr.Begin("hung", "srv", TypeBackup) // never finishes: the agent "restarts"
	j, _, _ := tr.Begin("done", "srv", TypeBackup)
	j.Done(nil, "ok")
	tr2 := New(st, func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error { return nil }, time.Now)
	if err := tr2.FailInterrupted(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if rec, _ := st.GetJob("hung"); rec.Status != failed || !strings.Contains(rec.Message, "interrupted") || rec.FinishedUnixMs == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("hung = %+v", rec)
	}
	if rec, _ := st.GetJob("done"); rec.Status != ok {
		t.Fatalf("done = %+v", rec)
	}
}

func TestLogIsBoundedKeepingTheEnd(t *testing.T) {
	tr, st, _, _ := setup(t)
	j, _, _ := tr.Begin("j1", "srv", TypeBackup)
	line := strings.Repeat("ñ", 511) // 1022 bytes: the trim may fall in the middle of a character
	for i := 0; i < 80; i++ {
		j.Log(line)
	}
	j.Log("LAST")
	rec, _ := st.GetJob("j1")
	if len(rec.Log) > MaxLog || !strings.HasPrefix(rec.Log, "…(trimmed)") || !strings.HasSuffix(rec.Log, "LAST\n") || !utf8.ValidString(rec.Log) {
		t.Fatalf("log of %d bytes, prefix %q", len(rec.Log), rec.Log[:20])
	}
}

func TestInventoryPrunesFiltersAndOrders(t *testing.T) {
	tr, _, _, c := setup(t)
	a, _, _ := tr.Begin("old", "s1", TypeBackup)
	a.Done(nil, "ok")
	c.t = c.t.Add(8 * 24 * time.Hour)
	b, _, _ := tr.Begin("s1-new", "s1", TypeBackup)
	b.Log(strings.Repeat("x", 10_000))
	c.t = c.t.Add(time.Minute)
	tr.Begin("s2-new", "s2", TypeRestore)
	all, err := tr.Inventory("")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(all) != 2 || all[0].JobId != "s2-new" || all[1].JobId != "s1-new" {
		t.Fatalf("inventory = %+v", all)
	}
	if len(all[1].Log) > inventoryLog {
		t.Fatalf("inventory log not trimmed: %d", len(all[1].Log))
	}
	only, _ := tr.Inventory("s1")
	if len(only) != 1 || only[0].JobId != "s1-new" {
		t.Fatalf("filtered = %+v", only)
	}
}

func TestNewIDIsUUIDv4(t *testing.T) {
	id := NewID()
	if len(id) != 36 || id[14] != '4' || id == NewID() {
		t.Fatalf("id = %s", id)
	}
}
```

`agent/internal/jobs/jobs.go`:
```go
// Package jobs records the agent's long jobs (backup, restore): it persists them in the
// store to answer a repeated job_id without repeating the work (spec §5.4) and emits
// JobProgress to the panel.
package jobs

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
)

const (
	TypeBackup  = "backup"
	TypeRestore = "restore"

	// MaxLog is the log kept per job (the end is kept).
	MaxLog = 64 << 10
	// KeepFor is how long finished jobs are remembered.
	KeepFor = 7 * 24 * time.Hour
	// progressStep: a smaller progress is not emitted (do not flood the WS with small files).
	progressStep = 5
	// inventoryJobs and inventoryLog bound the size of the inventory sent to the panel.
	inventoryJobs = 200
	inventoryLog  = 4 << 10
)

// Store is the part of the agent's store used by the jobs.
type Store interface {
	PutJob(*mcsmv1.JobRecord) error
	GetJob(id string) (*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)
	ListJobs() ([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error)
	PruneJobs(before time.Time) (int, error)
}

// Tracker creates and follows jobs.
type Tracker struct {
	st   Store
	send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error
	now  func() time.Time
	log  *slog.Logger
	mu   sync.Mutex // serializes Begin: two simultaneous duplicates do not start two runs
}

// New creates a Tracker. send must tolerate being disconnected (it returns an error that is ignored).
func New(st Store, send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error, now func() time.Time) *Tracker {
	return &Tracker{st: st, send: send, now: now, log: slog.Default().With("component", "jobs")}
}

// NewID generates an id (UUID v4) for the jobs started by the agent itself (scheduled backups).
func NewID() string {
	var b [16]byte
	_, _ = rand.Read(b[:])
	b[6] = b[6]&0x0f | 0x40
	b[8] = b[8]&0x3f | 0x80
	return fmt.Sprintf("%x-%x-%x-%x-%x", b[0:4], b[4:6], b[6:8], b[8:10], b[10:16])
}

// Begin records a new job as RUNNING and announces it. If the id already exists it returns
// (nil, saved record, nil): the caller must NOT run it again.
func (t *Tracker) Begin(id, serverID, typ string) (*Job, *mcsmv1.JobRecord, error) {
	if id == "" {
		return nil, nil, errors.New("job without id")
	}
	t.mu.Lock()
	defer t.mu.Unlock()
	old, err := t.st.GetJob(id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if old != nil {
		return nil, old, nil
	}
	rec := &mcsmv1.JobRecord{JobId: id, ServerId: serverID, Type: typ, Status: mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING, StartedUnixMs: t.now().UnixMilli()}
	if err := t.st.PutJob(rec); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	j := &Job{t: t, rec: rec}
	j.emit("")
	return j, nil, nil
}

// Replay resends to the panel the saved state of a job (answer to a repeated job_id).
func (t *Tracker) Replay(rec *mcsmv1.JobRecord) {
	_ = t.send(progressEnv(rec, "", t.now()))
}

// FailInterrupted marks as FAILED the jobs left RUNNING: the agent restarted in the middle.
func (t *Tracker) FailInterrupted() error {
	list, err := t.st.ListJobs()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, rec := range list {
		if rec.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING {
			continue
		}
		rec.Status = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_FAILED
		rec.Message = "interrupted: the agent restarted during the job"
		rec.FinishedUnixMs = t.now().UnixMilli()
		if err := t.st.PutJob(rec); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// Inventory returns the recent jobs for the panel (newest first, at most 200 and with the
// log trimmed to 4 KiB) after forgetting those finished more than KeepFor ago.
// Empty serverID = all.
func (t *Tracker) Inventory(serverID string) ([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, error) {
	if _, err := t.st.PruneJobs(t.now().Add(-KeepFor)); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	list, err := t.st.ListJobs()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]*mcsmv1.JobRecord, 0, len(list))
	for _, r := range list {
		if serverID == "" || r.ServerId == serverID {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].StartedUnixMs > out[j].StartedUnixMs })
	if len(out) > inventoryJobs {
		out = out[:inventoryJobs]
	}
	for _, r := range out {
		r.Log = tail(r.Log, inventoryLog)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// Job is a job in progress. Its methods are safe for concurrent use.
type Job struct {
	t        *Tracker
	mu       sync.Mutex
	rec      *mcsmv1.JobRecord
	lastSent uint32
	done     bool
}

// ID returns the job id (it does not change).
func (j *Job) ID() string { return j.rec.JobId }

// Progress updates the progress (0-99: Done sets 100). It is only saved and emitted if the
// message changes or the progress rises at least 5 points. The progress never goes back.
func (j *Job) Progress(pct uint32, msg string) {
	if pct > 99 {
		pct = 99
	}
	msg = clean(msg)
	j.mu.Lock()
	defer j.mu.Unlock()
	if j.done {
		return
	}
	if pct < j.rec.Progress {
		pct = j.rec.Progress
	}
	changed := msg != j.rec.Message || pct >= j.lastSent+progressStep
	j.rec.Progress, j.rec.Message = pct, msg
	if !changed {
		return
	}
	j.persist()
	j.emit("")
}

// Log adds a line to the job's log; it is saved and emitted as log_chunk.
func (j *Job) Log(line string) {
	chunk := clean(strings.TrimRight(line, "\n")) + "\n"
	j.mu.Lock()
	defer j.mu.Unlock()
	if j.done {
		return
	}
	j.rec.Log = tail(j.rec.Log+chunk, MaxLog)
	j.persist()
	j.emit(chunk)
}

// Done closes the job: OK with okMsg if err is nil, FAILED with the error if not. Idempotent.
func (j *Job) Done(err error, okMsg string) {
	j.mu.Lock()
	defer j.mu.Unlock()
	if j.done {
		return
	}
	j.done = true
	if err != nil {
		j.rec.Status = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_FAILED
		j.rec.Message = clean(err.Error())
	} else {
		j.rec.Status = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK
		j.rec.Progress = 100
		j.rec.Message = clean(okMsg)
	}
	j.rec.FinishedUnixMs = j.t.now().UnixMilli()
	j.persist()
	j.emit("")
}

// Record returns a copy of the current state.
func (j *Job) Record() *mcsmv1.JobRecord {
	j.mu.Lock()
	defer j.mu.Unlock()
	return proto.Clone(j.rec).(*mcsmv1.JobRecord)
}

func (j *Job) persist() {
	if err := j.t.st.PutJob(j.rec); err != nil {
		j.t.log.Warn("save job", "job", j.rec.JobId, "err", err)
	}
}

func (j *Job) emit(chunk string) {
	j.lastSent = j.rec.Progress
	_ = j.t.send(progressEnv(j.rec, chunk, j.t.now()))
}

func progressEnv(r *mcsmv1.JobRecord, chunk string, now time.Time) *mcsmv1.Envelope {
	return &mcsmv1.Envelope{Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_JobProgress{JobProgress: &mcsmv1.JobProgress{
		JobId: r.JobId, ServerId: r.ServerId, Type: r.Type, Status: r.Status, Progress: r.Progress,
		Message: r.Message, LogChunk: chunk, TsUnixMs: now.UnixMilli(),
	}}}
}

// clean leaves s as valid UTF-8: proto3 rejects invalid strings when serializing, and file
// names or RCON output may carry loose bytes.
func clean(s string) string { return strings.ToValidUTF8(s, "�") }

// tail trims s to its last max bytes (marking the cut) without splitting a character.
func tail(s string, max int) string {
	if len(s) <= max {
		return s
	}
	const mark = "…(trimmed)\n"
	cut := s[len(s)-max+len(mark):]
	if i := strings.IndexByte(cut, '\n'); i >= 0 && i < len(cut)-1 {
		cut = cut[i+1:]
	}
	return mark + strings.ToValidUTF8(cut, "")
}
```

### Task 4: `tar.zst` archive with exclusions and safe extraction

**Agent:** systems-expert (streaming tar.zst and zip-slip).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/backup/archive.go`, `agent/internal/backup/archive_test.go`
- Modify: `agent/go.mod`/`go.sum`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `backup.NewExcluder(patterns []string) (*Excluder, error)`, `(*Excluder).Excluded(rel string) bool` (no `/` → name at any depth; with `/` → exact relative path; an excluded directory is skipped whole); `type backup.ArchiveStats struct{ Files int; Bytes int64; Warnings []string }`; `type backup.ProgressFunc func(done, total int64)`; `backup.WriteArchive(ctx, src string, w io.Writer, excl *Excluder, progress ProgressFunc) (ArchiveStats, error)` (does not follow symlinks; a file that shrinks → padded with zeros + warning, one that grows → truncated to the scanned size, deleted → skipped + warning); `backup.ExtractArchive(ctx, r io.Reader, dst string, warn func(string)) error` (`dst` must exist and be empty; rejects absolute paths or paths outside `dst`; does not create symlinks, hardlinks or special files; restores permissions, mtime and, as root, the owner).
- Test helpers reused by Tasks 5 and 6 (same package): `mustWrite(t, path, body, mode)`, `mustRead(t, path) string`, `exists(path) bool`, `makeArchive(t, []tarEntry) []byte`.
- Note: depending on the Go version, `tar.Reader.Next` may itself return `ErrInsecurePath` for `../`; in both cases `ExtractArchive` fails without writing anything outside. The test covers it.

`agent/internal/backup/archive_test.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"archive/tar"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/klauspost/compress/zstd"
)

// mustWrite creates p (and its directories) with body and permissions mode.
func mustWrite(t *testing.T, p, body string, mode fs.FileMode) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(body), mode); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Chmod(p, mode); err != nil { // no umask
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func mustRead(t *testing.T, p string) string {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := os.ReadFile(p)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return string(b)
}

func exists(p string) bool {
	_, err := os.Lstat(p)
	return err == nil
}

type tarEntry struct {
	hdr  tar.Header
	body string
}

// makeArchive builds a tar.zst by hand (to test malicious entries).
func makeArchive(t *testing.T, entries []tarEntry) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	enc, err := zstd.NewWriter(&buf)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tw := tar.NewWriter(enc)
	for _, e := range entries {
		h := e.hdr
		if h.Typeflag == tar.TypeReg {
			h.Size = int64(len(e.body))
		}
		if h.Mode == 0 {
			h.Mode = 0o644
		}
		if err := tw.WriteHeader(&h); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if e.body != "" {
			if _, err := tw.Write([]byte(e.body)); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}
	}
	if err := tw.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := enc.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return buf.Bytes()
}

func TestExcluder(t *testing.T) {
	e, err := NewExcluder([]string{"logs", "*.tmp", "world/session.lock", "cache/"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cases := map[string]bool{
		"logs": true, "plugins/foo/logs": true, "a/b.tmp": true, "x.tmp": true,
		"world/session.lock": true, "other/session.lock": false, "cache": true,
		"cache2": false, "world/level.dat": false, "logs.txt": false,
	}
	for rel, want := range cases {
		if got := e.Excluded(rel); got != want {
			t.Errorf("Excluded(%q) = %v, want %v", rel, got, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := NewExcluder([]string{"[abc"}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a malformed pattern must be rejected")
	}
	if (*Excluder)(nil).Excluded("x") {
		t.Fatal("without exclusions nothing is excluded")
	}
}

func TestArchiveRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	src := t.TempDir()
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "server.properties"), "motd=hola\n", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "world", "level.dat"), "LEVEL", 0o640)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "world", "region", "r.0.0.mca"), strings.Repeat("x", 200_000), 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "world", "session.lock"), "lock", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "logs", "latest.log"), "log", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "plugins", "foo", "logs", "a.log"), "nested log", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "tmp", "file.tmp"), "tmp", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "empty.txt"), "", 0o644)
	if err := os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(src, "emptydir"), 0o750); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Chmod(filepath.Join(src, "emptydir"), 0o750); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Symlink("server.properties", filepath.Join(src, "link.properties")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	mtime := time.Date(2025, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 0, time.UTC)
	if err := os.Chtimes(filepath.Join(src, "world", "level.dat"), mtime, mtime); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	excl, err := NewExcluder([]string{"logs", "*.tmp", "world/session.lock"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	var lastDone, lastTotal int64
	st, err := WriteArchive(context.Background(), src, &buf, excl, func(d, tot int64) { lastDone, lastTotal = d, tot })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantBytes := int64(len("motd=hola\n") + len("LEVEL") + 200_000)
	if lastTotal != wantBytes || lastDone != wantBytes || st.Bytes != wantBytes {
		t.Fatalf("progress %d/%d, bytes %d; expected %d", lastDone, lastTotal, st.Bytes, wantBytes)
	}
	if st.Files != 4 { // server.properties, level.dat, r.0.0.mca, empty.txt
		t.Fatalf("files = %d", st.Files)
	}

	dst := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "out")
	if err := os.Mkdir(dst, 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var warns []string
	if err := ExtractArchive(context.Background(), &buf, dst, func(w string) { warns = append(warns, w) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dst, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL" || len(mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dst, "world", "region", "r.0.0.mca"))) != 200_000 {
		t.Fatal("different content after extracting")
	}
	for _, gone := range []string{"logs", "plugins/foo/logs", "tmp/file.tmp", "world/session.lock", "link.properties"} {
		if exists(filepath.Join(dst, gone)) {
			t.Errorf("%s must not be restored", gone)
		}
	}
	if !exists(filepath.Join(dst, "tmp")) || !exists(filepath.Join(dst, "empty.txt")) {
		t.Fatal("the (empty) tmp directory and empty.txt must exist")
	}
	if fi, _ := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dst, "world", "level.dat")); fi.Mode().Perm() != 0o640 || !fi.ModTime().Equal(mtime) {
		t.Fatalf("level.dat: mode %v, mtime %v", fi.Mode().Perm(), fi.ModTime())
	}
	if fi, _ := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dst, "emptydir")); fi.Mode().Perm() != 0o750 {
		t.Fatalf("emptydir: mode %v", fi.Mode().Perm())
	}
	if len(warns) != 1 || !strings.Contains(warns[0], "link.properties") {
		t.Fatalf("warnings = %v", warns)
	}
}

func TestExtractRejectsPathsOutsideDestination(t *testing.T) {
	for _, name := range []string{"../evil.txt", "a/../../evil.txt", "ABS"} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			base := t.TempDir()
			dst := filepath.Join(base, "dst")
			if err := os.Mkdir(dst, 0o755); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			n := name
			if n == "ABS" {
				n = filepath.Join(base, "evil.txt") // absolute path
			}
			data := makeArchive(t, []tarEntry{
				{hdr: tar.Header{Name: "ok.txt", Typeflag: tar.TypeReg}, body: "ok"},
				{hdr: tar.Header{Name: n, Typeflag: tar.TypeReg}, body: "pwned"},
			})
			err := ExtractArchive(context.Background(), bytes.NewReader(data), dst, nil)
			if err == nil {
				t.Fatal("must reject the backup")
			}
			if exists(filepath.Join(base, "evil.txt")) {
				t.Fatal("written outside the destination")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestExtractNeverFollowsLinks(t *testing.T) {
	base := t.TempDir()
	outside := filepath.Join(base, "outside")
	dst := filepath.Join(base, "dst")
	for _, d := range []string{outside, dst} {
		if err := os.Mkdir(d, 0o755); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	data := makeArchive(t, []tarEntry{
		{hdr: tar.Header{Name: "link", Typeflag: tar.TypeSymlink, Linkname: outside}},
		{hdr: tar.Header{Name: "link/pwned.txt", Typeflag: tar.TypeReg}, body: "pwned"},
		{hdr: tar.Header{Name: "hard", Typeflag: tar.TypeLink, Linkname: "/etc/passwd"}},
	})
	var warns []string
	if err := ExtractArchive(context.Background(), bytes.NewReader(data), dst, func(w string) { warns = append(warns, w) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if exists(filepath.Join(outside, "pwned.txt")) {
		t.Fatal("written through a symbolic link")
	}
	if fi, err := os.Lstat(filepath.Join(dst, "link")); err != nil || fi.Mode()&fs.ModeSymlink != 0 || !fi.IsDir() {
		t.Fatalf("link must be a real directory inside the destination: %v %v", fi, err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dst, "link", "pwned.txt")) != "pwned" || exists(filepath.Join(dst, "hard")) {
		t.Fatal("unexpected content")
	}
	if len(warns) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("warnings = %v", warns)
	}
}

func TestExtractCorruptArchive(t *testing.T) {
	data := makeArchive(t, []tarEntry{{hdr: tar.Header{Name: "a.txt", Typeflag: tar.TypeReg}, body: strings.Repeat("a", 100_000)}})
	dst := t.TempDir()
	err := ExtractArchive(context.Background(), bytes.NewReader(data[:len(data)/2]), dst, nil)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a truncated backup must return an error")
	}
}

func TestWriteArchiveCancelled(t *testing.T) {
	src := t.TempDir()
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(src, "a.txt"), "a", 0o644)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()
	if _, err := WriteArchive(ctx, src, io.Discard, nil, nil); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestWriteEntryPadsShrunkFileAndTruncatesGrown(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	shrunk, grown := filepath.Join(dir, "shrunk.log"), filepath.Join(dir, "grown.log")
	mustWrite(t, shrunk, "0123456789", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, grown, "0123456789", 0o644)
	si, _ := os.Lstat(shrunk)
	gi, _ := os.Lstat(grown)
	mustWrite(t, shrunk, "0123", 0o644)                 // shrinks after the scan
	mustWrite(t, grown, "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJ", 0o644) // grows after the scan
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	tw := tar.NewWriter(&buf)
	n, skipped, warn, err := writeEntry(tw, entry{rel: "shrunk.log", abs: shrunk, info: si}, make([]byte, 64))
	if err != nil || skipped || n != 10 || !strings.Contains(warn, "changed") {
		t.Fatalf("shrunk: n=%d skipped=%v warn=%q err=%v", n, skipped, warn, err)
	}
	n, _, warn, err = writeEntry(tw, entry{rel: "grown.log", abs: grown, info: gi}, make([]byte, 64))
	if err != nil || n != 10 || warn != "" {
		t.Fatalf("grown: n=%d warn=%q err=%v", n, warn, err)
	}
	if _, _, warn, _ = writeEntry(tw, entry{rel: "gone.log", abs: filepath.Join(dir, "gone.log"), info: si}, make([]byte, 64)); !strings.Contains(warn, "deleted") {
		t.Fatalf("gone: warn=%q", warn)
	}
	if err := tw.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tr := tar.NewReader(&buf)
	want := []string{"0123\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00", "0123456789"}
	for _, w := range want {
		if _, err := tr.Next(); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(tr)
		if string(body) != w {
			t.Fatalf("content %q, expected %q", body, w)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implementation**

`agent/internal/backup/archive.go`:
```go
// Package backup creates, lists, prunes and restores backups of a server's data (spec §6.5).
package backup

import (
	"archive/tar"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/klauspost/compress/zstd"
)

// Excluder decides which paths stay out of the backup. Paths are relative to the data root
// and use `/`. A pattern without `/` is matched against the name at any depth ("logs",
// "*.tmp"); one with `/` against the full relative path ("world/session.lock").
// An excluded directory is skipped whole.
type Excluder struct{ base, full []string }

// NewExcluder validates the patterns (path.Match syntax).
func NewExcluder(patterns []string) (*Excluder, error) {
	e := &Excluder{}
	for _, p := range patterns {
		p = strings.Trim(p, "/")
		if p == "" {
			continue
		}
		if _, err := path.Match(p, ""); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid exclude pattern %q: %w", p, err)
		}
		if strings.Contains(p, "/") {
			e.full = append(e.full, p)
		} else {
			e.base = append(e.base, p)
		}
	}
	return e, nil
}

// Excluded reports whether the relative path rel is left out of the backup.
func (e *Excluder) Excluded(rel string) bool {
	if e == nil {
		return false
	}
	name := path.Base(rel)
	for _, p := range e.base {
		if ok, _ := path.Match(p, name); ok {
			return true
		}
	}
	for _, p := range e.full {
		if ok, _ := path.Match(p, rel); ok {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// ArchiveStats summarizes a backup.
type ArchiveStats struct {
	Files    int
	Bytes    int64    // uncompressed content
	Warnings []string // entries skipped or that changed during the backup
}

// ProgressFunc receives the content bytes copied so far and the expected total.
type ProgressFunc func(done, total int64)

type entry struct {
	rel  string
	abs  string
	info fs.FileInfo
	link string
}

// WriteArchive writes the content of src (without the directory itself) as a tar compressed
// with zstd. It does not follow symbolic links: it stores them as links. A file that grows
// during the backup is truncated to its initial size and one that shrinks is padded with
// zeros (it is listed in Warnings): the backup is not aborted because a log is being written.
func WriteArchive(ctx context.Context, src string, w io.Writer, excl *Excluder, progress ProgressFunc) (ArchiveStats, error) {
	var st ArchiveStats
	entries, total, err := scan(src, excl, &st)
	if err != nil {
		return st, err
	}
	enc, err := zstd.NewWriter(w, zstd.WithEncoderLevel(zstd.SpeedDefault))
	if err != nil {
		return st, err
	}
	tw := tar.NewWriter(enc)
	finished := false
	defer func() {
		if !finished {
			_ = tw.Close()
			_ = enc.Close()
		}
	}()
	buf := make([]byte, 1<<20)
	var done int64
	for _, e := range entries {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return st, err
		}
		n, skipped, warn, err := writeEntry(tw, e, buf)
		if err != nil {
			return st, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", e.rel, err)
		}
		if warn != "" {
			st.Warnings = append(st.Warnings, warn)
		}
		if e.info.Mode().IsRegular() && !skipped {
			st.Files++
		}
		done += n
		if progress != nil {
			progress(done, total)
		}
	}
	finished = true
	if err := tw.Close(); err != nil {
		_ = enc.Close()
		return st, err
	}
	if err := enc.Close(); err != nil {
		return st, err
	}
	st.Bytes = done
	return st, nil
}

// scan walks src in lexical order applying the exclusions; it returns the entries and the
// total size of the regular files.
func scan(src string, excl *Excluder, st *ArchiveStats) ([]entry, int64, error) {
	var out []entry
	var total int64
	err := filepath.WalkDir(src, func(p string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err != nil {
			if p != src && errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
				return nil // disappeared during the walk
			}
			return err
		}
		if p == src {
			return nil
		}
		rel, err := filepath.Rel(src, p)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		rel = filepath.ToSlash(rel)
		if excl.Excluded(rel) {
			if d.IsDir() {
				return filepath.SkipDir
			}
			return nil
		}
		info, err := d.Info()
		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
				return nil
			}
			return err
		}
		e := entry{rel: rel, abs: p, info: info}
		switch {
		case info.Mode()&fs.ModeSymlink != 0:
			if e.link, err = os.Readlink(p); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		case info.Mode().IsRegular():
			total += info.Size()
		case info.IsDir():
		default:
			st.Warnings = append(st.Warnings, "skipped (not a file or directory): "+rel)
			return nil
		}
		out = append(out, e)
		return nil
	})
	return out, total, err
}

func writeEntry(tw *tar.Writer, e entry, buf []byte) (n int64, skipped bool, warn string, err error) {
	hdr, err := tar.FileInfoHeader(e.info, e.link)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, false, "", err
	}
	hdr.Name = e.rel
	if e.info.IsDir() {
		hdr.Name += "/"
	}
	hdr.Format = tar.FormatPAX
	hdr.Uname, hdr.Gname = "", ""
	if !e.info.Mode().IsRegular() {
		return 0, false, "", tw.WriteHeader(hdr)
	}
	f, err := os.Open(e.abs)
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
			return 0, true, "skipped (deleted during the backup): " + e.rel, nil
		}
		return 0, false, "", err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	if err := tw.WriteHeader(hdr); err != nil {
		return 0, false, "", err
	}
	n, err = io.CopyBuffer(tw, io.LimitReader(f, hdr.Size), buf)
	if err != nil {
		return n, false, "", err
	}
	if n < hdr.Size {
		if _, err := io.CopyN(tw, zeroReader{}, hdr.Size-n); err != nil {
			return n, false, "", err
		}
		warn = "changed during the backup (padded with zeros): " + e.rel
		n = hdr.Size
	}
	return n, false, warn, nil
}

type zeroReader struct{}

func (zeroReader) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	clear(p)
	return len(p), nil
}

// ExtractArchive decompresses into dst, which must exist and be empty. It rejects entries with
// an absolute path or that leave dst (zip-slip) and does not create symbolic links, hard links
// or special files (it skips them and reports through warn): without links, no later entry
// can write outside dst. It restores permissions and dates and, if the agent runs as root,
// the owner (the itzg image uses uid 1000).
func ExtractArchive(ctx context.Context, r io.Reader, dst string, warn func(string)) error {
	if warn == nil {
		warn = func(string) {}
	}
	root, err := filepath.Abs(dst)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	dec, err := zstd.NewReader(r)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer dec.Close()
	tr := tar.NewReader(dec)
	type dirMeta struct {
		path  string
		mode  fs.FileMode
		mtime time.Time
	}
	var dirs []dirMeta
	buf := make([]byte, 1<<20)
	for {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		hdr, err := tr.Next()
		if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
			break
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("unreadable or damaged backup: %w", err)
		}
		target, err := safeJoin(root, hdr.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		mode := fs.FileMode(hdr.Mode).Perm()
		switch hdr.Typeflag {
		case tar.TypeDir:
			if err := os.MkdirAll(target, 0o755); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			chown(target, hdr)
			dirs = append(dirs, dirMeta{target, mode, hdr.ModTime})
		case tar.TypeReg:
			if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(target), 0o755); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := writeFile(target, tr, mode, buf); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			chown(target, hdr)
			_ = os.Chtimes(target, hdr.ModTime, hdr.ModTime)
		default:
			warn(fmt.Sprintf("skipped %s: links and special files are not restored", hdr.Name))
		}
	}
	// Directory permissions and dates at the end: writing inside changes their mtime.
	for i := len(dirs) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		_ = os.Chmod(dirs[i].path, dirs[i].mode)
		_ = os.Chtimes(dirs[i].path, dirs[i].mtime, dirs[i].mtime)
	}
	return nil
}

func writeFile(target string, r io.Reader, mode fs.FileMode, buf []byte) error {
	f, err := os.OpenFile(target, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := io.CopyBuffer(f, r, buf); err != nil {
		f.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := f.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.Chmod(target, mode)
}

// safeJoin resolves name inside root or fails if it escapes (zip-slip).
func safeJoin(root, name string) (string, error) {
	if name == "" || strings.ContainsRune(name, 0) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("entry with invalid name %q: backup rejected", name)
	}
	p := filepath.FromSlash(name)
	if filepath.IsAbs(p) || filepath.VolumeName(p) != "" {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("entry with absolute path %q: backup rejected", name)
	}
	target := filepath.Join(root, p)
	if target != root && !strings.HasPrefix(target, root+string(filepath.Separator)) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("entry outside the destination %q: backup rejected", name)
	}
	return target, nil
}

func chown(p string, hdr *tar.Header) {
	if os.Geteuid() == 0 {
		_ = os.Lchown(p, hdr.Uid, hdr.Gid)
	}
}
```

```go
package backup

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
)

func TestFilenameAndParse(t *testing.T) {
	at := time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 4, 0, 0, 123e6, time.FixedZone("CEST", 2*3600))
	name := Filename("survival", at, 7)
	if name != "survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst" {
		t.Fatalf("name = %s", name)
	}
	if !ValidName("survival", name) {
		t.Fatal("the generated name must be valid")
	}
	ts, v, ok := parseName("survival", name)
	if !ok || !ts.Equal(at) || v != 7 {
		t.Fatalf("parse = %v %d %v", ts, v, ok)
	}
}

func TestValidName(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[string]bool{
		"survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst":           true,
		"survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst.json":      false,
		"survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst.partial":   false,
		"otro-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst":               false,
		"survival-x-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst":         false,
		"../survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst":        false,
		"survival-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst/../../etc": false,
		"survival-20261012T020000Z-7.tar.zst":               false,
	}
	for name, want := range cases {
		if got := ValidName("survival", name); got != want {
			t.Errorf("ValidName(%q) = %v", name, got)
		}
	}
	if ValidName("../x", "../x-20261012T020000.123Z-7.tar.zst") {
		t.Fatal("an invalid slug never gives valid names")
	}
}

func TestPreRestoreName(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir := "/srv/minecraft/survival/data"
	name := PreRestoreName(dataDir, time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 5, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC))
	if name != "data.pre-restore-20261012T050000.000Z" || !ValidPreRestoreName(dataDir, name) {
		t.Fatalf("name = %s", name)
	}
	for _, bad := range []string{"data", "data.pre-restore-", "data.pre-restore-../x", "backups", "data.restoring-20261012T050000.000Z"} {
		if ValidPreRestoreName(dataDir, bad) {
			t.Errorf("%q must not be valid", bad)
		}
	}
}

func TestListAndMeta(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	dir := filepath.Join(root, "survival", "backups")
	t1 := time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	t2 := t1.Add(time.Hour)
	a := Filename("survival", t1, 3)
	b := Filename("survival", t2, 4)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, a), "AAAA", 0o640)
	if err := WriteMeta(dir, Meta{Filename: a, ServerID: "s1", Slug: "survival", SpecVersion: 3, Trigger: TriggerScheduled, SHA256: "abc", StartedAt: t1, FinishedAt: t1}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, b), "BBBBBB", 0o640) // without .json
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, Filename("survival", t2.Add(time.Hour), 5)+".partial"), "x", 0o640)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, Filename("otro", t2, 1)), "x", 0o640)
	list, err := List(dir, "survival")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(list) != 2 || list[0].Filename != b || list[1].Filename != a {
		t.Fatalf("list = %+v", list)
	}
	if list[0].SizeBytes != 6 || list[0].SpecVersion != 4 || list[0].Trigger != TriggerManual || !list[0].StartedAt.Equal(t2) {
		t.Fatalf("deduced from the name = %+v", list[0])
	}
	if list[1].SHA256 != "abc" || list[1].Trigger != TriggerScheduled || list[1].SizeBytes != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("with .json = %+v", list[1])
	}
	if got, err := List(filepath.Join(root, "nada"), "survival"); err != nil || got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("nonexistent directory = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	info := list[1].Info("s1")
	if info.ServerId != "s1" || info.Trigger != mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_SCHEDULED || info.SizeBytes != 4 || info.StartedUnixMs != t1.UnixMilli() {
		t.Fatalf("info = %+v", info)
	}
}

func TestListPreRestoreAndDelete(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	dataDir := filepath.Join(root, "survival", "data")
	backupsDir := filepath.Join(root, "survival", "backups")
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "level.dat"), "actual", 0o644)
	pre := PreRestoreName(dataDir, time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 5, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC))
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(root, "survival", pre, "level.dat"), "antes", 0o644)
	if err := writeJSON(filepath.Join(root, "survival", pre+MetaExt), Meta{SizeBytes: 5}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	list, err := ListPreRestore(dataDir)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 || list[0].Filename != pre || list[0].Trigger != TriggerPreRestore || list[0].SizeBytes != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("pre-restore = %+v, %v", list, err)
	}
	name := Filename("survival", time.Now(), 1)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(backupsDir, name), "x", 0o640)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(backupsDir, name+MetaExt), "{}", 0o640)

	for _, bad := range []string{"data", "../data", "backups", "data.pre-restore-x", name + "/.."} {
		if err := Delete(backupsDir, dataDir, "survival", bad); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("Delete(%q) must fail", bad)
		}
	}
	if !exists(dataDir) || !exists(backupsDir) {
		t.Fatal("an invalid name must not delete anything")
	}
	if err := Delete(backupsDir, dataDir, "survival", name); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if exists(filepath.Join(backupsDir, name)) || exists(filepath.Join(backupsDir, name+MetaExt)) {
		t.Fatal("the backup and its .json must be deleted")
	}
	if err := Delete(backupsDir, dataDir, "survival", name); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("deleting twice is idempotent: %v", err)
	}
	if err := Delete(backupsDir, dataDir, "survival", pre); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if exists(filepath.Join(root, "survival", pre)) || exists(filepath.Join(root, "survival", pre+MetaExt)) || !exists(filepath.Join(dataDir, "level.dat")) {
		t.Fatal("only the pre-restore directory must be deleted")
	}
	_ = os.RemoveAll(root)
}

func TestPrunePlan(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 10, 20, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	m := func(name, trigger string, daysAgo int) Meta {
		return Meta{Filename: name, Trigger: trigger, StartedAt: now.Add(-time.Duration(daysAgo) * 24 * time.Hour)}
	}
	metas := []Meta{
		m("s0", TriggerScheduled, 0), m("s1", TriggerScheduled, 1), m("s2", TriggerScheduled, 2),
		m("s9", TriggerScheduled, 9), m("manual-old", TriggerManual, 30), m("pre", TriggerPreRestore, 30),
	}
	cases := []struct {
		days, count int
		want        []string
	}{
		{0, 0, nil},
		{0, 2, []string{"s2", "s9"}},
		{7, 0, []string{"s9"}},
		{7, 3, []string{"s9"}},
		{1, 10, []string{"s2", "s9"}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := PrunePlan(metas, c.days, c.count, now); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, c.want) {
			t.Errorf("keepDays=%d keepCount=%d: %v, want %v", c.days, c.count, got, c.want)
		}
	}
	old := []Meta{m("a", TriggerScheduled, 40), m("b", TriggerScheduled, 50)}
	if got := PrunePlan(old, 7, 0, now); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"b"}) {
		t.Fatalf("the most recent one is never deleted: %v", got)
	}
}

func TestTriggerConversion(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tr := range []string{TriggerManual, TriggerScheduled, TriggerPreMigration, TriggerPreRestore} {
		if TriggerFromProto(TriggerToProto(tr)) != tr {
			t.Errorf("round trip of %s", tr)
		}
	}
	if TriggerFromProto(mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_UNSPECIFIED) != TriggerManual {
		t.Fatal("UNSPECIFIED counts as manual")
	}
}
```
