- [ ] **Step 2: Implementation**

`agent/internal/backup/files.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/spec"
)

const (
	// Ext is the extension of backups.
	Ext = ".tar.zst"
	// MetaExt is the extension of the metadata file that accompanies each backup.
	MetaExt    = ".json"
	partialExt = ".partial"
	// tsLayout is basic ISO 8601 in UTC with milliseconds (no `:`: a portable name).
	tsLayout        = "20060102T150405.000Z"
	preRestoreInfix = ".pre-restore-"
	restoringInfix  = ".restoring-"

	TriggerManual       = "manual"
	TriggerScheduled    = "scheduled"
	TriggerPreMigration = "pre_migration"
	TriggerPreRestore   = "pre_restore"
)

var (
	nameTailRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^(\d{8}T\d{6}\.\d{3}Z)-(\d{1,19})\.tar\.zst$`)
	tsRe       = regexp.MustCompile(`^\d{8}T\d{6}\.\d{3}Z$`)
)

// Meta is the metadata of a backup: the .json next to the .tar.zst (and next to each
// pre-restore directory).
type Meta struct {
	Filename    string    `json:"filename"`
	ServerID    string    `json:"serverId"`
	Slug        string    `json:"slug"`
	SpecVersion uint64    `json:"specVersion"`
	Trigger     string    `json:"trigger"` // manual | scheduled | pre_migration | pre_restore
	SizeBytes   int64     `json:"sizeBytes"`
	SHA256      string    `json:"sha256"`
	StartedAt   time.Time `json:"startedAt"`
	FinishedAt  time.Time `json:"finishedAt"`
	Excludes    []string  `json:"excludes,omitempty"`
	Files       int       `json:"files,omitempty"`
}

// Dir is the backups directory of a server: <dataRoot>/<slug>/backups.
func Dir(dataRoot, slug string) string { return filepath.Join(dataRoot, slug, "backups") }

// Filename is <slug>-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSS.mmmZ>-<spec_version>.tar.zst (spec §6.5).
func Filename(slug string, t time.Time, specVersion uint64) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s-%d%s", slug, t.UTC().Format(tsLayout), specVersion, Ext)
}

// ValidName reports whether name is a .tar.zst backup of that slug. A valid name never
// contains `/` or `..`.
func ValidName(slug, name string) bool {
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(name, slug+"-")
	return ok && spec.ValidSlug(slug) && nameTailRe.MatchString(rest)
}

// PreRestoreName is the name of the directory where the data previous to a restore is kept,
// next to dataDir: e.g. data.pre-restore-20261012T040000.000Z.
func PreRestoreName(dataDir string, t time.Time) string {
	return filepath.Base(dataDir) + preRestoreInfix + t.UTC().Format(tsLayout)
}

// ValidPreRestoreName reports whether name is a pre-restore directory of dataDir.
func ValidPreRestoreName(dataDir, name string) bool {
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(name, filepath.Base(dataDir)+preRestoreInfix)
	return ok && tsRe.MatchString(rest)
}

func parseName(slug, name string) (time.Time, uint64, bool) {
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(name, slug+"-")
	if !ok {
		return time.Time{}, 0, false
	}
	m := nameTailRe.FindStringSubmatch(rest)
	if m == nil {
		return time.Time{}, 0, false
	}
	ts, err := time.Parse(tsLayout, m[1])
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, 0, false
	}
	v, err := strconv.ParseUint(m[2], 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, 0, false
	}
	return ts, v, true
}

// WriteMeta writes <dir>/<m.Filename>.json atomically.
func WriteMeta(dir string, m Meta) error {
	return writeJSON(filepath.Join(dir, m.Filename+MetaExt), m)
}

func writeJSON(p string, v any) error {
	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(v, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmp := p + partialExt
	if err := os.WriteFile(tmp, b, 0o640); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.Rename(tmp, p); err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(tmp)
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

// ReadMeta reads <dir>/<name>.json.
func ReadMeta(dir, name string) (Meta, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, name+MetaExt))
	if err != nil {
		return Meta{}, err
	}
	var m Meta
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &m); err != nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("unreadable metadata of %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	m.Filename = name
	return m, nil
}

// List returns the .tar.zst backups of a server, newest first. A backup without .json (or
// with an unreadable one) is listed with what is deduced from the name. It ignores the
// .partial files (backups in progress or aborted). A nonexistent directory is an empty list.
func List(backupsDir, slug string) ([]Meta, error) {
	ents, err := os.ReadDir(backupsDir)
	if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Meta
	for _, e := range ents {
		name := e.Name()
		if !e.Type().IsRegular() || !ValidName(slug, name) {
			continue
		}
		m, err := ReadMeta(backupsDir, name)
		if err != nil {
			ts, ver, _ := parseName(slug, name)
			m = Meta{Filename: name, Slug: slug, SpecVersion: ver, Trigger: TriggerManual, StartedAt: ts, FinishedAt: ts}
		}
		if info, err := e.Info(); err == nil {
			m.SizeBytes = info.Size()
		}
		out = append(out, m)
	}
	sortNewest(out)
	return out, nil
}

// ListPreRestore returns the pre-restore directories next to dataDir.
func ListPreRestore(dataDir string) ([]Meta, error) {
	parent := filepath.Dir(dataDir)
	ents, err := os.ReadDir(parent)
	if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Meta
	for _, e := range ents {
		name := e.Name()
		if !e.IsDir() || !ValidPreRestoreName(dataDir, name) {
			continue
		}
		m, err := ReadMeta(parent, name)
		if err != nil {
			ts, _ := time.Parse(tsLayout, strings.TrimPrefix(name, filepath.Base(dataDir)+preRestoreInfix))
			m = Meta{StartedAt: ts, FinishedAt: ts}
		}
		m.Filename, m.Trigger = name, TriggerPreRestore
		out = append(out, m)
	}
	sortNewest(out)
	return out, nil
}

// Delete removes a backup (.tar.zst and its .json) or a pre-restore directory of dataDir.
// The name is validated before touching anything; it no longer existing is not an error.
func Delete(backupsDir, dataDir, slug, name string) error {
	switch {
	case ValidName(slug, name):
		for _, p := range []string{filepath.Join(backupsDir, name), filepath.Join(backupsDir, name+MetaExt)} {
			if err := os.Remove(p); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	case ValidPreRestoreName(dataDir, name):
		p := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(dataDir), name)
		if err := os.RemoveAll(p); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := os.Remove(p + MetaExt); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
			return err
		}
		return nil
	default:
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid backup name %q", name)
	}
}

// PrunePlan returns the backups to delete according to retention. Only the scheduled ones
// count (manual, pre_migration and pre_restore are deleted by hand); the most recent one is
// never deleted; 0 = no limit.
func PrunePlan(metas []Meta, keepDays, keepCount int, now time.Time) []string {
	var sched []Meta
	for _, m := range metas {
		if m.Trigger == TriggerScheduled {
			sched = append(sched, m)
		}
	}
	sortNewest(sched)
	var out []string
	for i, m := range sched {
		if i == 0 {
			continue
		}
		tooMany := keepCount > 0 && i >= keepCount
		tooOld := keepDays > 0 && now.Sub(m.StartedAt) > time.Duration(keepDays)*24*time.Hour
		if tooMany || tooOld {
			out = append(out, m.Filename)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func sortNewest(ms []Meta) {
	sort.SliceStable(ms, func(i, j int) bool {
		if !ms[i].StartedAt.Equal(ms[j].StartedAt) {
			return ms[i].StartedAt.After(ms[j].StartedAt)
		}
		return ms[i].Filename > ms[j].Filename
	})
}

// Info converts the metadata to the protocol message.
func (m Meta) Info(serverID string) *mcsmv1.BackupInfo {
	info := &mcsmv1.BackupInfo{
		ServerId: serverID, Filename: m.Filename, Sha256: m.SHA256, SpecVersion: m.SpecVersion,
		Trigger: TriggerToProto(m.Trigger),
	}
	if m.SizeBytes > 0 {
		info.SizeBytes = uint64(m.SizeBytes)
	}
	if !m.StartedAt.IsZero() {
		info.StartedUnixMs = m.StartedAt.UnixMilli()
	}
	if !m.FinishedAt.IsZero() {
		info.FinishedUnixMs = m.FinishedAt.UnixMilli()
	}
	return info
}

// TriggerToProto converts the trigger of the .json to the protocol enum.
func TriggerToProto(t string) mcsmv1.BackupTrigger {
	switch t {
	case TriggerScheduled:
		return mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_SCHEDULED
	case TriggerPreMigration:
		return mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_MIGRATION
	case TriggerPreRestore:
		return mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_RESTORE
	default:
		return mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_MANUAL
	}
}

// TriggerFromProto is the inverse; UNSPECIFIED counts as manual.
func TriggerFromProto(t mcsmv1.BackupTrigger) string {
	switch t {
	case mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_SCHEDULED:
		return TriggerScheduled
	case mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_MIGRATION:
		return TriggerPreMigration
	case mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_RESTORE:
		return TriggerPreRestore
	default:
		return TriggerManual
	}
}
```

### Task 6: Create backups with RCON and restore without deleting anything

**Agent:** systems-expert (RCON with guaranteed `save-on`, atomic restore).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/backup/create.go`, `agent/internal/backup/create_test.go`, `agent/internal/backup/restore.go`, `agent/internal/backup/restore_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 4 and 5.
- Produces: `backup.FlushWait = 3 * time.Second`; `backup.ErrResume`; `type backup.RconFunc func(ctx context.Context, command string) (string, error)`; `backup.Sleep(ctx, d) error`; `type backup.CreateRequest struct{ ServerID, Slug string; SpecVersion uint64; Trigger, DataDir, BackupsDir string; Excludes []string; Rcon RconFunc; Now func() time.Time; Sleep func(context.Context, time.Duration) error; Progress func(pct uint32, msg string); Log func(line string) }`; `backup.Create(ctx, CreateRequest) (Meta, error)` — with `Rcon` (server running): `save-off`, `save-all flush`, waits `FlushWait`, copies, and **always** `save-on` (defer registered before the `save-off`, own 15 s context); `Rcon == nil` copies directly; if it fails, the `.partial` is deleted; if only `save-on` fails, it returns the `Meta` **and** an error that wraps `ErrResume`.
- Produces: `type backup.RestoreRequest struct{ ServerID, Slug, Filename, DataDir, BackupsDir string; Now func() time.Time; Progress func(uint32, string); Log func(string) }`; `backup.Restore(ctx, RestoreRequest) (Meta, error)` — the server is already stopped; verifies sha256 if there is a `.json`, extracts into `<data>.restoring-<ts>`, renames `data` to `data.pre-restore-<ts>` (with its `.json`) and the extracted one to `data`; if anything fails before the swap, `data` stays intact and the temporary is deleted; it returns the `Meta` of the pre-restore (`Filename` empty if there was no data).
- Progress the user will see: 1 % disabling autosave, 2–97 % copying, 99 % written; restore 2 % verifying, 10–90 % extracting, 92 % moving the current data aside.

`agent/internal/backup/create_test.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

// fakeRcon records the commands and the state of the context each one arrived with.
type fakeRcon struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	cmds   []string
	ctxErr map[string]error
	fail   map[string]error
}

func (f *fakeRcon) run(ctx context.Context, cmd string) (string, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.cmds = append(f.cmds, cmd)
	if f.ctxErr == nil {
		f.ctxErr = map[string]error{}
	}
	f.ctxErr[cmd] = ctx.Err()
	return "ok", f.fail[cmd]
}

var t0 = time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

// newWorld creates <root>/survival/data with a minimal world and returns data and backups.
func newWorld(t *testing.T) (dataDir, backupsDir string) {
	t.Helper()
	root := t.TempDir()
	dataDir = filepath.Join(root, "survival", "data")
	backupsDir = filepath.Join(root, "survival", "backups")
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "server.properties"), "motd=hola\n", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat"), "LEVEL-1", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "logs", "latest.log"), "ruido", 0o644)
	return dataDir, backupsDir
}

func baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir string) CreateRequest {
	return CreateRequest{
		ServerID: "srv-1", Slug: "survival", SpecVersion: 3, Trigger: TriggerManual,
		DataDir: dataDir, BackupsDir: backupsDir, Excludes: []string{"logs"},
		Now:   func() time.Time { return t0 },
		Sleep: func(context.Context, time.Duration) error { return nil },
	}
}

// assertNoArchives checks that no backups or partials remain.
func assertNoArchives(t *testing.T, backupsDir string) {
	t.Helper()
	ents, _ := os.ReadDir(backupsDir)
	if len(ents) != 0 {
		names := []string{}
		for _, e := range ents {
			names = append(names, e.Name())
		}
		t.Fatalf("files remain: %v", names)
	}
}

func TestCreateRunningServerQuiescesWithRcon(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	rc := &fakeRcon{}
	req := baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)
	req.Rcon = rc.run
	var slept time.Duration
	req.Sleep = func(_ context.Context, d time.Duration) error { slept = d; return nil }
	var logs []string
	req.Log = func(l string) { logs = append(logs, l) }
	meta, err := Create(context.Background(), req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := strings.Join(rc.cmds, ","); got != "save-off,save-all flush,save-on" {
		t.Fatalf("RCON commands = %s", got)
	}
	if slept != FlushWait {
		t.Fatalf("wait after flush = %v", slept)
	}
	if meta.Filename != "survival-20261012T040000.000Z-3.tar.zst" || meta.Trigger != TriggerManual || meta.ServerID != "srv-1" {
		t.Fatalf("meta = %+v", meta)
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(backupsDir, meta.Filename))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
	if hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) != meta.SHA256 || int64(len(data)) != meta.SizeBytes {
		t.Fatal("sha256 or size does not match the file")
	}
	if onDisk, err := ReadMeta(backupsDir, meta.Filename); err != nil || onDisk.SHA256 != meta.SHA256 {
		t.Fatalf(".json = %+v, %v", onDisk, err)
	}
	out := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "x")
	if err := os.Mkdir(out, 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := ExtractArchive(context.Background(), bytes.NewReader(data), out, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(out, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-1" || exists(filepath.Join(out, "logs")) {
		t.Fatal("wrong backup content")
	}
	if ents, _ := os.ReadDir(backupsDir); len(ents) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("the .tar.zst and its .json must remain: %d", len(ents))
	}
	if !strings.Contains(strings.Join(logs, "\n"), "save-on") {
		t.Fatalf("the job log must record save-on: %v", logs)
	}
}

func TestCreateCancelledStillSendsSaveOn(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	rc := &fakeRcon{}
	req := baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)
	req.Rcon = rc.run
	req.Sleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error { cancel(); return ctx.Err() }
	if _, err := Create(ctx, req); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if rc.cmds[len(rc.cmds)-1] != "save-on" {
		t.Fatalf("commands = %v", rc.cmds)
	}
	if rc.ctxErr["save-on"] != nil {
		t.Fatal("save-on must go with a live context even if the job is cancelled")
	}
	assertNoArchives(t, backupsDir)
}

func TestCreateSaveOffFailureStillSendsSaveOn(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	rc := &fakeRcon{fail: map[string]error{"save-off": errors.New("rcon: connect: timeout")}}
	req := baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)
	req.Rcon = rc.run
	_, err := Create(context.Background(), req)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "save-off") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if got := strings.Join(rc.cmds, ","); got != "save-off,save-on" {
		t.Fatalf("commands = %s", got)
	}
	assertNoArchives(t, backupsDir)
}

func TestCreateFailureMidCopyRemovesPartial(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	rc := &fakeRcon{}
	req := baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)
	req.Rcon = rc.run
	req.Progress = func(pct uint32, _ string) {
		if pct > 2 { // after the first file
			cancel()
		}
	}
	if _, err := Create(ctx, req); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("must fail")
	}
	if rc.cmds[len(rc.cmds)-1] != "save-on" {
		t.Fatalf("commands = %v", rc.cmds)
	}
	assertNoArchives(t, backupsDir)
}

func TestCreateSaveOnFailureKeepsTheBackup(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	rc := &fakeRcon{fail: map[string]error{"save-on": errors.New("rcon: connect: connection refused")}}
	req := baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)
	req.Rcon = rc.run
	meta, err := Create(context.Background(), req)
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrResume) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if meta.Filename == "" || !exists(filepath.Join(backupsDir, meta.Filename)) {
		t.Fatal("the written backup is kept even if save-on fails")
	}
}

func TestCreateStoppedServerSkipsRcon(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	meta, err := Create(context.Background(), baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir)) // Rcon nil
	if err != nil || !exists(filepath.Join(backupsDir, meta.Filename)) {
		t.Fatalf("meta = %+v, %v", meta, err)
	}
}

func TestCreateWithoutData(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	req := baseReq(filepath.Join(root, "survival", "data"), filepath.Join(root, "survival", "backups"))
	if _, err := Create(context.Background(), req); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "has no data yet") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	req = baseReq(filepath.Join(root, "x"), filepath.Join(root, "y"))
	req.Slug = "../etc"
	if _, err := Create(context.Background(), req); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid slug must be rejected")
	}
}
```

```go
package backup

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func makeBackup(t *testing.T, dataDir, backupsDir string) Meta {
	t.Helper()
	m, err := Create(context.Background(), baseReq(dataDir, backupsDir))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return m
}

func restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, name string, at time.Time) RestoreRequest {
	return RestoreRequest{ServerID: "srv-1", Slug: "survival", Filename: name, DataDir: dataDir, BackupsDir: backupsDir, Now: func() time.Time { return at }}
}

// leftovers lists the temporary .restoring-* directories left behind.
func leftovers(t *testing.T, dataDir string) []string {
	t.Helper()
	ents, _ := os.ReadDir(filepath.Dir(dataDir))
	var out []string
	for _, e := range ents {
		if strings.Contains(e.Name(), restoringInfix) {
			out = append(out, e.Name())
		}
	}
	return out
}

func TestRestoreSwapsAndKeepsPrevious(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	m := makeBackup(t, dataDir, backupsDir)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat"), "LEVEL-2", 0o644)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "nuevo.txt"), "x", 0o644)
	pre, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, m.Filename, t0.Add(time.Hour)))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-1" || exists(filepath.Join(dataDir, "nuevo.txt")) {
		t.Fatal("the data is not the backup's")
	}
	if pre.Filename != "data.pre-restore-20261012T050000.000Z" || pre.Trigger != TriggerPreRestore || pre.SizeBytes == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("pre = %+v", pre)
	}
	prePath := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(dataDir), pre.Filename)
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(prePath, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-2" || !exists(filepath.Join(prePath, "nuevo.txt")) {
		t.Fatal("the previous data must be kept intact")
	}
	if list, _ := ListPreRestore(dataDir); len(list) != 1 || list[0].SizeBytes != pre.SizeBytes {
		t.Fatalf("ListPreRestore = %+v", list)
	}
	if l := leftovers(t, dataDir); len(l) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("leftovers: %v", l)
	}
}

func TestRestoreTwiceKeepsBothPrevious(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	m := makeBackup(t, dataDir, backupsDir)
	for i, at := range []time.Time{t0.Add(time.Hour), t0.Add(2 * time.Hour)} {
		if _, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, m.Filename, at)); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("restore %d: %v", i, err)
		}
	}
	if list, _ := ListPreRestore(dataDir); len(list) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("a previous pre-restore is never deleted: %+v", list)
	}
}

func TestRestoreCorruptBackupLeavesDataUntouched(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	m := makeBackup(t, dataDir, backupsDir)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat"), "LEVEL-2", 0o644)
	p := filepath.Join(backupsDir, m.Filename)
	data := []byte(mustRead(t, p))
	data[len(data)/2] ^= 0xff
	if err := os.WriteFile(p, data, 0o640); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, m.Filename, t0.Add(time.Hour)))
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "damaged") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-2" {
		t.Fatal("the current data must not be touched")
	}
	if list, _ := ListPreRestore(dataDir); len(list) != 0 || len(leftovers(t, dataDir)) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("no pre-restore directories or temporaries may remain")
	}
}

func TestRestoreUnreadableArchiveLeavesDataUntouched(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	m := makeBackup(t, dataDir, backupsDir)
	mustWrite(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat"), "LEVEL-2", 0o644)
	if err := os.Remove(filepath.Join(backupsDir, m.Filename+MetaExt)); err != nil { // without sha256 to verify
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	p := filepath.Join(backupsDir, m.Filename)
	data := mustRead(t, p)
	if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(data[:len(data)/2]), 0o640); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, m.Filename, t0.Add(time.Hour))); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a truncated backup must fail")
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-2" || len(leftovers(t, dataDir)) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("the current data must not be touched or temporaries left")
	}
}

func TestRestoreRejectsBadNames(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	for _, name := range []string{"../../etc/passwd", "otro-20261012T040000.000Z-3.tar.zst", "data.pre-restore-20261012T040000.000Z"} {
		if _, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, name, t0)); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%q must be rejected", name)
		}
	}
}

func TestRestoreWithoutPreviousData(t *testing.T) {
	dataDir, backupsDir := newWorld(t)
	m := makeBackup(t, dataDir, backupsDir)
	if err := os.RemoveAll(dataDir); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pre, err := Restore(context.Background(), restoreReq(dataDir, backupsDir, m.Filename, t0.Add(time.Hour)))
	if err != nil || pre.Filename != "" {
		t.Fatalf("pre = %+v, %v", pre, err)
	}
	if mustRead(t, filepath.Join(dataDir, "world", "level.dat")) != "LEVEL-1" {
		t.Fatal("data not restored")
	}
}
```

`agent/internal/backup/create.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"mcsm/agent/internal/spec"
)

const (
	// FlushWait is the wait after save-all flush (spec §6.5).
	FlushWait = 3 * time.Second
	// resumeTimeout bounds save-on, which is sent even if the job was cancelled.
	resumeTimeout = 15 * time.Second
)

// ErrResume indicates that the backup was made but autosave could not be re-enabled.
var ErrResume = errors.New("autosave could not be re-enabled (save-on); run save-on in the console")

// RconFunc runs a console command on the server.
type RconFunc func(ctx context.Context, command string) (string, error)

// CreateRequest describes a backup.
type CreateRequest struct {
	ServerID    string
	Slug        string
	SpecVersion uint64
	Trigger     string
	DataDir     string // <DATA_ROOT>/<slug>/data
	BackupsDir  string // <DATA_ROOT>/<slug>/backups
	Excludes    []string
	// Rcon is nil if the server is stopped: then it copies without save-off.
	Rcon     RconFunc
	Now      func() time.Time
	Sleep    func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error
	Progress func(pct uint32, msg string)
	Log      func(line string)
}

func (r *CreateRequest) defaults() {
	if r.Now == nil {
		r.Now = time.Now
	}
	if r.Sleep == nil {
		r.Sleep = Sleep
	}
	if r.Progress == nil {
		r.Progress = func(uint32, string) {}
	}
	if r.Log == nil {
		r.Log = func(string) {}
	}
}

// Sleep waits d or until ctx ends.
func Sleep(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
	t := time.NewTimer(d)
	defer t.Stop()
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	case <-t.C:
		return nil
	}
}

// Create makes a consistent backup (spec §6.5). With the server running: save-off,
// save-all flush, a 3 s wait, copy and ALWAYS save-on (in a defer, registered before the
// save-off and with its own context: neither a failure nor a cancellation leaves the world
// without autosave). The file is written as .partial and renamed at the end; if anything
// fails the partial is deleted (spec §10). If the backup was written but save-on fails, it
// returns the backup's metadata AND an error that wraps ErrResume.
func Create(ctx context.Context, req CreateRequest) (meta Meta, err error) {
	req.defaults()
	if !spec.ValidSlug(req.Slug) {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid slug %q", req.Slug)
	}
	excl, err := NewExcluder(req.Excludes)
	if err != nil {
		return Meta{}, err
	}
	if fi, serr := os.Stat(req.DataDir); serr != nil || !fi.IsDir() {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("the server has no data yet to copy (%s)", req.DataDir)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(req.BackupsDir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("create the backups directory: %w", err)
	}
	started := req.Now()
	name := Filename(req.Slug, started, req.SpecVersion)
	final := filepath.Join(req.BackupsDir, name)
	if _, serr := os.Stat(final); serr == nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("backup %s already exists", name)
	}

	if req.Rcon != nil {
		defer func() {
			rctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), resumeTimeout)
			defer cancel()
			if _, rerr := req.Rcon(rctx, "save-on"); rerr != nil {
				req.Log("WARNING: save-on failed: " + rerr.Error())
				if err == nil {
					err = fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrResume, rerr)
				}
				return
			}
			req.Log("save-on: autosave re-enabled")
		}()
		req.Progress(1, "Disabling autosave")
		if _, err := req.Rcon(ctx, "save-off"); err != nil {
			return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("RCON save-off: %w", err)
		}
		req.Log("save-off: autosave disabled")
		if _, err := req.Rcon(ctx, "save-all flush"); err != nil {
			return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("RCON save-all flush: %w", err)
		}
		req.Log("save-all flush: world written to disk")
		if err := req.Sleep(ctx, FlushWait); err != nil {
			return Meta{}, err
		}
	}

	size, sum, stats, err := writeTo(ctx, req, excl, final)
	if err != nil {
		return Meta{}, err
	}
	for _, w := range stats.Warnings {
		req.Log(w)
	}
	meta = Meta{
		Filename: name, ServerID: req.ServerID, Slug: req.Slug, SpecVersion: req.SpecVersion, Trigger: req.Trigger,
		SizeBytes: size, SHA256: sum, StartedAt: started.UTC(), FinishedAt: req.Now().UTC(), Excludes: req.Excludes, Files: stats.Files,
	}
	if err := WriteMeta(req.BackupsDir, meta); err != nil {
		req.Log("warning: could not write the .json metadata (the backup is valid): " + err.Error())
	}
	req.Log(fmt.Sprintf("backup written: %s (%d files, %d compressed bytes)", name, stats.Files, size))
	return meta, nil
}

// writeTo writes the backup to <final>.partial computing its sha256, syncs it and renames
// it to final. If anything fails, it deletes the partial.
func writeTo(ctx context.Context, req CreateRequest, excl *Excluder, final string) (size int64, sum string, st ArchiveStats, err error) {
	partial := final + partialExt
	f, err := os.OpenFile(partial, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o640)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, fmt.Errorf("create the backup: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() {
		if err != nil {
			_ = f.Close()
			_ = os.Remove(partial)
		}
	}()
	h := sha256.New()
	req.Progress(2, "Copying data")
	st, err = WriteArchive(ctx, req.DataDir, io.MultiWriter(f, h), excl, func(done, total int64) {
		pct := uint32(97)
		if total > 0 {
			pct = 2 + uint32(done*95/total)
		}
		req.Progress(pct, "Copying data")
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, fmt.Errorf("copy the data: %w", err)
	}
	if err = f.Sync(); err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, err
	}
	info, err := f.Stat()
	if err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, err
	}
	if err = f.Close(); err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, err
	}
	if err = os.Rename(partial, final); err != nil {
		return 0, "", st, err
	}
	return info.Size(), hex.EncodeToString(h.Sum(nil)), st, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Restore implementation**

`agent/internal/backup/restore.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"syscall"
	"time"
)

// RestoreRequest describes a restore. The server must be stopped: the caller stops it and
// also holds it in the reconciler.
type RestoreRequest struct {
	ServerID   string
	Slug       string
	Filename   string // name of the .tar.zst backup in BackupsDir
	DataDir    string
	BackupsDir string
	Now        func() time.Time
	Progress   func(pct uint32, msg string)
	Log        func(line string)
}

func (r *RestoreRequest) defaults() {
	if r.Now == nil {
		r.Now = time.Now
	}
	if r.Progress == nil {
		r.Progress = func(uint32, string) {}
	}
	if r.Log == nil {
		r.Log = func(string) {}
	}
}

// Restore replaces DataDir with the content of a backup WITHOUT ever deleting the previous
// data (spec §6.5): it verifies the sha256 if there is one, extracts into <data>.restoring-<ts>,
// renames DataDir to <data>.pre-restore-<ts> and the extracted one to DataDir. If anything
// fails before the swap, DataDir stays intact and the temporary directory is deleted. It
// returns the metadata of the pre-restore directory (empty Filename if there was no previous data).
func Restore(ctx context.Context, req RestoreRequest) (Meta, error) {
	req.defaults()
	if !ValidName(req.Slug, req.Filename) {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid backup name %q", req.Filename)
	}
	src := filepath.Join(req.BackupsDir, req.Filename)
	fi, err := os.Stat(src)
	if err != nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("backup %s is not on the node: %w", req.Filename, err)
	}
	if m, err := ReadMeta(req.BackupsDir, req.Filename); err == nil && m.SHA256 != "" {
		req.Progress(2, "Verifying the backup (sha256)")
		sum, err := fileSHA256(ctx, src)
		if err != nil {
			return Meta{}, err
		}
		if sum != m.SHA256 {
			return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("the backup is damaged: sha256 %s, expected %s", sum, m.SHA256)
		}
		req.Log("sha256 verified")
	} else {
		req.Log("warning: the backup has no saved sha256; restoring without verification")
	}

	now := req.Now()
	staging := req.DataDir + restoringInfix + now.UTC().Format(tsLayout)
	if err := os.Mkdir(staging, 0o755); err != nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("prepare the restore: %w", err)
	}
	swapped := false
	defer func() {
		if !swapped {
			_ = os.RemoveAll(staging) // our own freshly created directory: deleting it is safe
		}
	}()
	f, err := os.Open(src)
	if err != nil {
		return Meta{}, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	req.Progress(10, "Extracting the backup")
	pr := &progressReader{r: ctxReader{ctx: ctx, r: f}, total: max(fi.Size(), 1), fn: func(done, total int64) {
		req.Progress(10+uint32(done*80/total), "Extracting the backup")
	}}
	if err := ExtractArchive(ctx, pr, staging, req.Log); err != nil {
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("extract the backup: %w", err)
	}

	preName := PreRestoreName(req.DataDir, now)
	prePath := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(req.DataDir), preName)
	hadData := false
	cur, err := os.Stat(req.DataDir)
	switch {
	case err == nil && cur.IsDir():
		matchOwner(staging, cur)
		req.Progress(92, "Keeping the current data in "+preName)
		if err := os.Rename(req.DataDir, prePath); err != nil {
			return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("move the current data aside: %w", err)
		}
		hadData = true
	case err == nil:
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("%s exists and is not a directory", req.DataDir)
	case !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist):
		return Meta{}, err
	}
	if err := os.Rename(staging, req.DataDir); err != nil {
		if hadData {
			if uerr := os.Rename(prePath, req.DataDir); uerr != nil {
				return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("activate the backup: %v; the original data could not be restored either (it is still in %s): %v", err, prePath, uerr)
			}
		}
		return Meta{}, fmt.Errorf("activate the backup: %w", err)
	}
	swapped = true
	req.Log("backup restored to " + req.DataDir)
	if !hadData {
		return Meta{}, nil
	}
	pre := Meta{
		Filename: preName, ServerID: req.ServerID, Slug: req.Slug, Trigger: TriggerPreRestore,
		SizeBytes: dirSize(prePath), StartedAt: now.UTC(), FinishedAt: req.Now().UTC(),
	}
	if err := writeJSON(prePath+MetaExt, pre); err != nil {
		req.Log("warning: could not write the .json of " + preName + ": " + err.Error())
	}
	req.Log("previous data kept in " + preName + " (it is not deleted automatically)")
	return pre, nil
}

type progressReader struct {
	r     io.Reader
	done  int64
	total int64
	fn    func(done, total int64)
}

func (p *progressReader) Read(b []byte) (int, error) {
	n, err := p.r.Read(b)
	p.done += int64(n)
	p.fn(min(p.done, p.total), p.total)
	return n, err
}

type ctxReader struct {
	ctx context.Context
	r   io.Reader
}

func (c ctxReader) Read(p []byte) (int, error) {
	if err := c.ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return c.r.Read(p)
}

func fileSHA256(ctx context.Context, p string) (string, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(p)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	h := sha256.New()
	if _, err := io.Copy(h, ctxReader{ctx: ctx, r: f}); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return hex.EncodeToString(h.Sum(nil)), nil
}

// matchOwner copies the permissions and (if the agent is root) the owner of the original
// data directory to the restored one: the itzg image expects /data owned by uid 1000.
func matchOwner(p string, like fs.FileInfo) {
	_ = os.Chmod(p, like.Mode().Perm())
	if st, ok := like.Sys().(*syscall.Stat_t); ok && os.Geteuid() == 0 {
		_ = os.Lchown(p, int(st.Uid), int(st.Gid))
	}
}

func dirSize(root string) int64 {
	var n int64
	_ = filepath.WalkDir(root, func(_ string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
		if err == nil && d.Type().IsRegular() {
			if info, ierr := d.Info(); ierr == nil {
				n += info.Size()
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	return n
}
```

- Produces: `type backup.Clock interface{ Now() time.Time; Until(t time.Time) <-chan time.Time }`; `backup.RealClock`; `backup.NewScheduler(clock Clock, fire func(serverID string)) *Scheduler`; `(*Scheduler).Set(serverID, expr string) error` (same expression = no rescheduling; `""` = unschedule), `Remove(serverID)`, `IDs() []string`, `Next(serverID) (time.Time, bool)`, `Run(ctx)` (`fire` is called from the loop: it must return right away; each wait lasts at most 1 min; a missed run is not recovered); `backup.ShouldRun(onlyIfPlayersSince bool, lastBackup, playersSeen, watchingSince time.Time) bool`; `backup.NewPlayerWatch(now func() time.Time) *PlayerWatch` with `Observe(serverID, playersOnline uint32)`, `LastSeen(serverID) time.Time`, `Since() time.Time`.

`agent/internal/backup/schedule_test.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

type fakeWaiter struct {
	at time.Time
	ch chan time.Time
}

// fakeClock advances by hand. registered receives a notice each time the loop starts waiting.
type fakeClock struct {
	mu         sync.Mutex
	now        time.Time
	waiters    []fakeWaiter
	registered chan struct{}
}

func newFakeClock(t time.Time) *fakeClock {
	return &fakeClock{now: t, registered: make(chan struct{}, 1024)}
}

func (c *fakeClock) Now() time.Time {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.now
}

func (c *fakeClock) Until(at time.Time) <-chan time.Time {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	ch := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	if !at.After(c.now) {
		ch <- c.now
	} else {
		c.waiters = append(c.waiters, fakeWaiter{at, ch})
	}
	c.registered <- struct{}{}
	return ch
}

func (c *fakeClock) Advance(d time.Duration) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.now = c.now.Add(d)
	keep := c.waiters[:0]
	for _, w := range c.waiters {
		if !w.at.After(c.now) {
			w.ch <- c.now
		} else {
			keep = append(keep, w)
		}
	}
	c.waiters = keep
}

// waitLoop waits for the loop to sleep again (the timeout is only a safety net).
func (c *fakeClock) waitLoop(t *testing.T) {
	t.Helper()
	select {
	case <-c.registered:
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the scheduler did not wait again")
	}
}

func expectFire(t *testing.T, ch <-chan string, want string) {
	t.Helper()
	select {
	case got := <-ch:
		if got != want {
			t.Fatalf("fired %s, expected %s", got, want)
		}
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
		t.Fatalf("%s did not fire", want)
	}
}

func expectNoFire(t *testing.T, ch <-chan string) {
	t.Helper()
	select {
	case got := <-ch:
		t.Fatalf("unexpected fire of %s", got)
	default:
	}
}

func TestSchedulerFiresEveryMinute(t *testing.T) {
	clk := newFakeClock(time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 10, 0, 30, 0, time.UTC))
	fired := make(chan string, 10)
	s := NewScheduler(clk, func(id string) { fired <- id })
	if err := s.Set("a", "* * * * *"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if next, _ := s.Next("a"); !next.Equal(time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 10, 1, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("next = %v", next)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go s.Run(ctx)
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	clk.Advance(30 * time.Second)
	expectFire(t, fired, "a")
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	expectFire(t, fired, "a")
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	expectNoFire(t, fired)
}

func TestSchedulerDailyAndReschedule(t *testing.T) {
	clk := newFakeClock(time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 10, 0, 30, 0, time.UTC))
	fired := make(chan string, 10)
	s := NewScheduler(clk, func(id string) { fired <- id })
	if err := s.Set("b", "0 4 * * *"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go s.Run(ctx)
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	clk.Advance(time.Hour) // 11:00:30: nothing to do
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	expectNoFire(t, fired)
	clk.Advance(17 * time.Hour) // 04:00:30 the next day
	expectFire(t, fired, "b")
	clk.waitLoop(t)

	before, _ := s.Next("b")
	if err := s.Set("b", "0 4 * * *"); err != nil { // same expression: nothing changes
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if after, _ := s.Next("b"); !after.Equal(before) {
		t.Fatalf("same expression must not reschedule: %v → %v", before, after)
	}
	if err := s.Set("b", "*/5 * * * *"); err != nil { // wakes the loop
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	if next, _ := s.Next("b"); !next.Equal(time.Date(2026, 10, 13, 4, 5, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("next after rescheduling = %v", next)
	}
	if err := s.Set("b", "every day"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid expression")
	}
	s.Remove("b")
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	if ids := s.IDs(); len(ids) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("ids = %v", ids)
	}
	clk.Advance(10 * time.Minute)
	clk.waitLoop(t)
	expectNoFire(t, fired)
}

func TestShouldRun(t *testing.T) {
	start := time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	last := start.Add(time.Hour)
	cases := []struct {
		name          string
		only          bool
		last, seen, w time.Time
		want          bool
	}{
		{"without onlyIfPlayersSince always", false, last, time.Time{}, start, true},
		{"first backup", true, time.Time{}, time.Time{}, start, true},
		{"nobody since the last one", true, last, time.Time{}, start, false},
		{"players before the last one", true, last, last.Add(-time.Minute), start, false},
		{"players after the last one", true, last, last.Add(time.Minute), start, true},
		{"agent restarted after the last one", true, last, time.Time{}, last.Add(time.Minute), true},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := ShouldRun(c.only, c.last, c.seen, c.w); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", c.name, got)
		}
	}
}

func TestPlayerWatch(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	w := NewPlayerWatch(func() time.Time { return now })
	if !w.Since().Equal(now) || !w.LastSeen("a").IsZero() {
		t.Fatal("initial state")
	}
	now = now.Add(time.Minute)
	w.Observe("a", 0)
	if !w.LastSeen("a").IsZero() {
		t.Fatal("0 players does not count")
	}
	w.Observe("a", 2)
	if !w.LastSeen("a").Equal(now) {
		t.Fatalf("LastSeen = %v", w.LastSeen("a"))
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implementation**

`agent/internal/backup/schedule.go`:
```go
package backup

import (
	"context"
	"sort"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/robfig/cron/v3"
)

// Clock is the scheduler's clock (injectable in tests).
type Clock interface {
	Now() time.Time
	// Until returns a channel that receives a value when the clock reaches t.
	Until(t time.Time) <-chan time.Time
}

type realClock struct{}

func (realClock) Now() time.Time                     { return time.Now() }
func (realClock) Until(t time.Time) <-chan time.Time { return time.After(time.Until(t)) }

// RealClock is the system clock. Cron expressions are interpreted in its time zone (TZ
// variable of the agent container; UTC if not set).
var RealClock Clock = realClock{}

// maxSchedWait bounds each wait: a jump of the system clock is corrected within 1 min.
const maxSchedWait = time.Minute

type schedEntry struct {
	expr  string
	sched cron.Schedule
	next  time.Time
}

// Scheduler fires fire(serverID) according to each server's cron expression. It uses
// cron.ParseStandard from robfig/cron to compute the next run and its own loop with an
// injectable clock (robfig's runner uses the real clock and cannot be tested). A missed run
// (agent stopped) is not recovered.
type Scheduler struct {
	clock   Clock
	fire    func(serverID string)
	mu      sync.Mutex
	entries map[string]*schedEntry
	wake    chan struct{}
}

// NewScheduler creates the scheduler. fire is called from the loop: it must return right away.
func NewScheduler(clock Clock, fire func(serverID string)) *Scheduler {
	return &Scheduler{clock: clock, fire: fire, entries: map[string]*schedEntry{}, wake: make(chan struct{}, 1)}
}

// Set schedules a server or reschedules it if the expression changed (if it did not change,
// it keeps the next run). An empty expression unschedules it.
func (s *Scheduler) Set(serverID, expr string) error {
	if expr == "" {
		s.Remove(serverID)
		return nil
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if e, ok := s.entries[serverID]; ok && e.expr == expr {
		return nil
	}
	sched, err := cron.ParseStandard(expr)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.entries[serverID] = &schedEntry{expr: expr, sched: sched, next: sched.Next(s.clock.Now())}
	s.poke()
	return nil
}

// Remove unschedules a server.
func (s *Scheduler) Remove(serverID string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if _, ok := s.entries[serverID]; ok {
		delete(s.entries, serverID)
		s.poke()
	}
}

// IDs returns the scheduled servers, sorted.
func (s *Scheduler) IDs() []string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]string, 0, len(s.entries))
	for id := range s.entries {
		out = append(out, id)
	}
	sort.Strings(out)
	return out
}

// Next returns the next run of a server.
func (s *Scheduler) Next(serverID string) (time.Time, bool) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	e, ok := s.entries[serverID]
	if !ok {
		return time.Time{}, false
	}
	return e.next, true
}

func (s *Scheduler) poke() {
	select {
	case s.wake <- struct{}{}:
	default:
	}
}

// Run executes the loop until ctx ends.
func (s *Scheduler) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	for {
		now := s.clock.Now()
		wakeAt := now.Add(maxSchedWait)
		var due []string
		s.mu.Lock()
		for id, e := range s.entries {
			if !e.next.After(now) {
				due = append(due, id)
				e.next = e.sched.Next(now)
			}
			if e.next.Before(wakeAt) {
				wakeAt = e.next
			}
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
		sort.Strings(due)
		for _, id := range due {
			s.fire(id)
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-s.wake:
		case <-s.clock.Until(wakeAt):
		}
	}
}

// ShouldRun decides whether a scheduled backup is made (onlyIfPlayersSince, spec §6.5). It is
// skipped only if: there is a previous backup, the agent has observed the server since before
// that backup and nobody has connected since then. When in doubt (agent restarted), it is made.
func ShouldRun(onlyIfPlayersSince bool, lastBackup, playersSeen, watchingSince time.Time) bool {
	if !onlyIfPlayersSince || lastBackup.IsZero() {
		return true
	}
	if watchingSince.After(lastBackup) {
		return true
	}
	return playersSeen.After(lastBackup)
}

// PlayerWatch remembers when a player was last seen on each server (with each observed
// state, every ~10 s). In memory only: Since indicates since when it observes.
type PlayerWatch struct {
	now   func() time.Time
	since time.Time
	mu    sync.Mutex
	seen  map[string]time.Time
}

// NewPlayerWatch starts observing now.
func NewPlayerWatch(now func() time.Time) *PlayerWatch {
	return &PlayerWatch{now: now, since: now(), seen: map[string]time.Time{}}
}

// Observe records the number of connected players of a server.
func (w *PlayerWatch) Observe(serverID string, playersOnline uint32) {
	if playersOnline == 0 {
		return
	}
	t := w.now()
	w.mu.Lock()
	w.seen[serverID] = t
	w.mu.Unlock()
}

// LastSeen is the last time the server had players (zero if never since Since).
func (w *PlayerWatch) LastSeen(serverID string) time.Time {
	w.mu.Lock()
	defer w.mu.Unlock()
	return w.seen[serverID]
}

// Since is since when it is observing.
func (w *PlayerWatch) Since() time.Time { return w.since }
```

### Task 8: Server hold in the reconciler (`Hold`)

**Agent:** systems-expert (per-server lock of the reconciler).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/reconcile/hold.go`, `agent/internal/reconcile/hold_test.go`
- Modify: `agent/internal/reconcile/reconciler.go` (field `held` and three checks; outside an anchor, marked in the diff)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `(*Reconciler).lock`, `reconcileLocked`, `Power`, `Remove`, test helpers `setup`, `put`, `calls`, `specJSON` (Plan 1).
- Produces: `var reconcile.ErrBusy`; `(*Reconciler).Hold(serverID, reason string) (release func(ctx context.Context), err error)`; `(*Reconciler).Held(serverID string) bool`. Semantics: `Hold` takes the per-server lock (waits for the operation in progress), marks the hold and releases the lock; while it lasts, `reconcileLocked` (Apply, ApplyForce, Reconcile, ReconcileTick) only keeps the spec saved and returns without touching Docker or `SetApplied`; `Power` and `Remove` return `ErrBusy` (wrapped with the reason) without touching anything; a second `Hold` returns `ErrBusy`. `release(ctx)` (idempotent) removes the hold and reconciles: it applies the deferred spec and returns the server to its desired state.
- Lock order: per-server lock → `r.mu` (the same one `reconcileTick` already uses); never the other way round.

```go
package reconcile

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
)

const specJSON3G = `{"imageTag":"java21","type":"PAPER","version":"1.21.4","memory":"3G","ports":{"game":25565},"restartPolicy":"unless-stopped","rconPassword":"abcdefgh"}`

const runningState = mcsmv1.DesiredState_DESIRED_STATE_RUNNING

func TestHoldDefersApplyUntilRelease(t *testing.T) {
	r, st, fake, _, _ := setup(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	put(t, st, "a", "alpha", 1, runningState, specJSON)
	if err := r.Reconcile(ctx, "a"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	release, err := r.Hold("a", "backup")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before := len(fake.Calls)
	if err := r.Apply(ctx, &mcsmv1.ServerSpec{ServerId: "a", Slug: "alpha", Version: 2, DesiredState: runningState, SpecJson: []byte(specJSON3G)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r.ReconcileAll(ctx)
	for _, c := range fake.Calls[before:] {
		if strings.HasPrefix(c, "Stop") || strings.HasPrefix(c, "Remove") || strings.HasPrefix(c, "Create") {
			t.Fatalf("must not touch the container during the hold: %v", fake.Calls[before:])
		}
	}
	if v, _ := st.Applied("a"); v != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("applied = %d, want 1", v)
	}
	if sp, _ := st.GetSpec("a"); sp.Version != 2 {
		t.Fatal("the new spec must be saved")
	}
	release(ctx)
	if v, _ := st.Applied("a"); v != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("after releasing applied = %d, want 2", v)
	}
	if calls(fake, "Remove") != 1 || calls(fake, "Create") != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("must recreate on release: %v", fake.Calls)
	}
	release(ctx) // idempotent
	if calls(fake, "Create") != 2 {
		t.Fatal("releasing twice repeats nothing")
	}
}

func TestHoldRejectsSecondHoldPowerAndRemove(t *testing.T) {
	r, st, fake, _, _ := setup(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	put(t, st, "a", "alpha", 1, runningState, specJSON)
	if err := r.Reconcile(ctx, "a"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	release, err := r.Hold("a", "restore")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !r.Held("a") || r.Held("b") {
		t.Fatal("Held")
	}
	if _, err := r.Hold("a", "backup"); !errors.Is(err, ErrBusy) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "restore") {
		t.Fatalf("second hold = %v", err)
	}
	stops := calls(fake, "Stop")
	if err := r.Power(ctx, "a", mcsmv1.PowerActionType_POWER_ACTION_TYPE_STOP); !errors.Is(err, ErrBusy) {
		t.Fatalf("Power = %v", err)
	}
	if calls(fake, "Stop") != stops {
		t.Fatal("Power must not touch the held container")
	}
	if err := r.Remove(ctx, "a", false); !errors.Is(err, ErrBusy) {
		t.Fatalf("Remove = %v", err)
	}
	if sp, _ := st.GetSpec("a"); sp == nil {
		t.Fatal("Remove must not delete the spec of a held server")
	}
	release(ctx)
	if r.Held("a") {
		t.Fatal("must be released")
	}
	if err := r.Power(ctx, "a", mcsmv1.PowerActionType_POWER_ACTION_TYPE_STOP); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestHoldWaitsForOperationInProgress(t *testing.T) {
	r, st, _, _, _ := setup(t)
	put(t, st, "a", "alpha", 1, runningState, specJSON)
	unlock := r.lock("a") // another operation on the server in progress
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		_, err := r.Hold("a", "backup")
		done <- err
	}()
	select {
	case <-done:
		t.Fatal("Hold must not return while another operation holds the server lock")
	default:
	}
	unlock()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Hold did not return after releasing the lock")
	}
}

func TestReleaseRestartsServerStoppedDuringHold(t *testing.T) {
	r, st, fake, _, _ := setup(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	put(t, st, "a", "alpha", 1, runningState, specJSON)
	if err := r.Reconcile(ctx, "a"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	release, err := r.Hold("a", "restore")
	if err != nil {

	if err := fake.Stop(ctx, cid, StopTimeout); err != nil { // the restore stops it
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r.ReconcileAll(ctx)
	if ins, _ := fake.Inspect(ctx, cid); ins.Running {
		t.Fatal("during the hold nobody must start it")
	}
	release(ctx)
	if ins, _ := fake.Inspect(ctx, cid); !ins.Running {
		t.Fatal("on release it returns to its desired state (running)")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implementation**

`agent/internal/reconcile/hold.go`:
```go
package reconcile

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
)

// ErrBusy: the server has a backup or a restore in progress.
var ErrBusy = errors.New("the server is busy with another backup or restore")

// Hold reserves a server for a maintenance operation (backup, restore). While it lasts,
// reconciliation (ApplySpec, SyncSpecs, ticks) only saves the spec and is deferred —a spec
// change does not recreate the container in the middle of a backup— and Power and Remove
// fail with ErrBusy. Hold waits for the operation in progress on the server to end (it takes
// the per-server lock) and returns ErrBusy if it was already held.
//
// release removes the hold and reconciles the server: it applies the deferred spec and returns
// the server to its desired state (starts it if a restore stopped it). It is idempotent.
func (r *Reconciler) Hold(serverID, reason string) (release func(ctx context.Context), err error) {
	unlock := r.lock(serverID)
	defer unlock()
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	if cur, ok := r.held[serverID]; ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w (%s)", ErrBusy, cur)
	}
	if r.held == nil {
		r.held = map[string]string{}
	}
	r.held[serverID] = reason
	var once sync.Once
	return func(ctx context.Context) {
		once.Do(func() {
			unlock := r.lock(serverID)
			defer unlock()
			r.mu.Lock()
			delete(r.held, serverID)
			r.mu.Unlock()
			if err := r.reconcileLocked(ctx, serverID); err != nil {
				r.log.Warn("reconcile when releasing the hold", "server", serverID, "err", err)
			}
		})
	}, nil
}

// Held reports whether the server is held by Hold.
func (r *Reconciler) Held(serverID string) bool { return r.heldReason(serverID) != "" }

func (r *Reconciler) heldReason(serverID string) string {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return r.held[serverID]
}
```

In `agent/internal/reconcile/reconciler.go`:

1. In the `Reconciler` struct, below `lastErr map[string]string`:
```go
	held    map[string]string // servers held by Hold (backup/restore) and their reason
```
(no need to initialize it in `New`: `Hold` creates it and reading a nil map is valid).

2. At the start of `reconcileLocked`, **before** the `defer` that publishes the observed state:
```go

		// The spec is already saved: it will be applied when the hold is released (Hold).
		r.log.Info("reconciliation deferred", "server", serverID, "reason", reason)
		return nil
	}
```

3. In `Remove`, right after `defer unlock()`:
```go
	if reason := r.heldReason(serverID); reason != "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w (%s)", ErrBusy, reason)
	}
```

4. In `Power`, right after `defer unlock()` and **before** the `defer` that publishes the state (a "busy" is not a server error and must not appear in `observed.error`):
```go
	if reason := r.heldReason(serverID); reason != "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w (%s)", ErrBusy, reason)
	}
```

- Consumes: Tasks 3–8; the `App` of Plan 1 (`a.st`, `a.eng`, `a.rec`, `a.rcon`, `a.send`, `a.cfg`, `a.log`, `ack`, `errMsg`); test helpers `newApp`, `specJSON` (`app_test.go`).
- Produces: `newBackupService(a *App, clock backup.Clock) *backupService` with `start(ctx)`, `refreshSchedules()`, `onSchedule(serverID)`, `handleBackupNow|Restore|Delete|List(id, msg, send)`, fields `jobs *jobs.Tracker`, `sched *backup.Scheduler`, `players *backup.PlayerWatch`, `sleep`, `wg sync.WaitGroup` (the tests wait for the jobs with `a.bk.wg.Wait()`); field `App.bk`.
- Behavior: `BackupNow` (trigger `MANUAL` by default or `PRE_MIGRATION`) → validates the server → `jobs.Begin` (repeated id: resends its state and `Ack`) → `rec.Hold` (busy: job `FAILED` + `Error{busy}`) → `Ack` → backup in the background with the agent's lifetime context (not the WS session's): running → RCON via `mcsm-<slug>:25575` with a 2 min deadline per command; stopped → direct copy; at the end `BackupCreated` (id 0), pruning (`BackupDeleted` with `reason: "prune"`), release of the hold and final `JobProgress`. `BackupRestore` → validates the name (before creating the job) → job → `Hold` → stops the container (confirms it is not running) → `backup.Restore` → `BackupCreated` of the pre-restore → release (reconciles and starts it if `desired_state=running`) → final job. `BackupDelete` → `backup.Delete` (busy if held). `BackupList` → `Ack` + `BackupInventory` (id 0) with backups, pre-restores and jobs. The scheduler syncs with the specs at startup and every 15 s; `onlyIfPlayersSince` uses the players of each `onObserved`. At startup, the jobs left RUNNING become FAILED "interrupted".
- `mc.Rcon` respects the context's deadline if it has one (before, it was cut at 5 s even if the caller asked for more): `SendCommand` now waits its 15 s and `save-all flush` its 2 min.

```go
// rconDeadline is the context's deadline if it has one (a save-all flush on a large world
// takes more than 5 s) or, otherwise, rconTimeout.
func rconDeadline(ctx context.Context, now time.Time) time.Time {
	if dl, ok := ctx.Deadline(); ok {
		return dl
	}
	return now.Add(rconTimeout)
}
```

```bash
cd agent && go test -race ./internal/mc/
```

`agent/internal/app/backups_test.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/backup"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/jobs"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/store"
)

// collector stores everything the agent sends without blocking (jobs emit a lot).
type collector struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	envs []*mcsmv1.Envelope
}

func (c *collector) send(e *mcsmv1.Envelope) error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.envs = append(c.envs, e)
	return nil
}

func (c *collector) all() []*mcsmv1.Envelope {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]*mcsmv1.Envelope(nil), c.envs...)
}

// resp returns the response (Ack or Error) to request id.
func (c *collector) resp(id uint64) *mcsmv1.Envelope {
	for _, e := range c.all() {
		if e.Id == id && (e.GetAck() != nil || e.GetError() != nil) {
			return e
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// final returns the last finished JobProgress (OK or FAILED) of a job.
func (c *collector) final(jobID string) *mcsmv1.JobProgress {
	var out *mcsmv1.JobProgress
	for _, e := range c.all() {
		if p := e.GetJobProgress(); p != nil && p.JobId == jobID && p.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING {
			out = p
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (c *collector) created() []*mcsmv1.BackupInfo {
	var out []*mcsmv1.BackupInfo
	for _, e := range c.all() {
		if m := e.GetBackupCreated(); m != nil {
			out = append(out, m.Backup)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (c *collector) deleted() []*mcsmv1.BackupDeleted {
	var out []*mcsmv1.BackupDeleted
	for _, e := range c.all() {
		if m := e.GetBackupDeleted(); m != nil {
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// manualClock: Until only fires if the time has already passed; the tests move the scheduler by hand.
type manualClock struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	now time.Time
}

func (c *manualClock) Now() time.Time {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.now
}

func (c *manualClock) Until(t time.Time) <-chan time.Time {
	ch := make(chan time.Time, 1)
	if !t.After(c.Now()) {
		ch <- t
	}
	return ch
}

func (c *manualClock) Advance(d time.Duration) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.now = c.now.Add(d)
}

// rconRec is a fake RCON. With block, save-all flush waits until it is closed (and signals through reached).
type rconRec struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	cmds    []string
	block   chan struct{}
	reached chan struct{}
}

func (r *rconRec) fn(ctx context.Context, host string, port uint16, password, cmd string) (string, error) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.cmds = append(r.cmds, cmd)
	block, reached := r.block, r.reached
	r.mu.Unlock()
	if cmd == "save-all flush" && block != nil {
		close(reached)
		select {
		case <-block:
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return "", ctx.Err()
		}
	}
	return "ok", nil
}

func (r *rconRec) list() []string {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), r.cmds...)
}

const backupsSpecJSON = `{"imageTag":"java21","type":"PAPER","version":"1.21.4","memory":"2G","ports":{"game":25565},"restartPolicy":"unless-stopped","rconPassword":"abcdefgh","backups":{"enabled":true,"cron":"* * * * *","keepDays":0,"keepCount":2,"excludes":["logs"],"onlyIfPlayersSince":false}}`

type backupEnv struct {
	a    *App
	fake *docker.Fake
	st   *store.Store
	col  *collector
	rc   *rconRec
	clk  *manualClock
}

// newBackupEnv: agent with server "a" (slug alpha) running and a world on disk.
func newBackupEnv(t *testing.T, specJS string) *backupEnv {
	t.Helper()
	a, fake, st := newApp(t)
	e := &backupEnv{a: a, fake: fake, st: st, col: &collector{}, rc: &rconRec{}, clk: &manualClock{now: time.Date(2026, 10, 12, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}}
	a.send = e.col.send
	a.rcon = e.rc.fn
	a.bk = newBackupService(a, e.clk)
	a.bk.sleep = func(context.Context, time.Duration) error { return nil }
	sp := &mcsmv1.ServerSpec{ServerId: "a", Slug: "alpha", Version: 1, DesiredState: mcsmv1.DesiredState_DESIRED_STATE_RUNNING, SpecJson: []byte(specJS)}
	a.Handle(context.Background(), &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: 1, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_ApplySpec{ApplySpec: &mcsmv1.ApplySpec{Server: sp}}}, e.col.send)
	if r := e.col.resp(1); r == nil || r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("ApplySpec: %+v", r)
	}
	e.writeLevel(t, "LEVEL-1")
	return e
}

func (e *backupEnv) dataDir() string    { return filepath.Join(e.a.cfg.DataRoot, "alpha", "data") }
func (e *backupEnv) backupsDir() string { return filepath.Join(e.a.cfg.DataRoot, "alpha", "backups") }

func (e *backupEnv) writeLevel(t *testing.T, level string) {
	t.Helper()
	p := filepath.Join(e.dataDir(), "world", "level.dat")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(p, []byte(level), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func (e *backupEnv) readLevel(t *testing.T, dir string) string {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, "world", "level.dat"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return string(b)
}

func (e *backupEnv) handle(id uint64, body any) *mcsmv1.Envelope {
	env := &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: id}
	switch b := body.(type) {
	case *mcsmv1.BackupNow:
		env.Body = &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupNow{BackupNow: b}
	case *mcsmv1.BackupRestore:
		env.Body = &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupRestore{BackupRestore: b}
	case *mcsmv1.BackupDelete:
		env.Body = &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupDelete{BackupDelete: b}
	case *mcsmv1.BackupList:
		env.Body = &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupList{BackupList: b}
	case *mcsmv1.ApplySpec:
		env.Body = &mcsmv1.Envelope_ApplySpec{ApplySpec: b}
	}
	e.a.Handle(context.Background(), env, e.col.send)
	return e.col.resp(id)
}

func fakeCalls(f *docker.Fake, prefix string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, c := range f.Calls {
		if strings.HasPrefix(c, prefix) {
			n++
		}
	}
	return n
}

func waitClosed(t *testing.T, ch <-chan struct{}) {
	t.Helper()
	select {
	case <-ch:
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the backup did not reach the expected point")
	}
}

const (
	jobOK     = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK
	jobFailed = mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_FAILED
)

func TestBackupNowOnRunningServer(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	if r := e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("resp = %+v", r)
	}
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if got := strings.Join(e.rc.list(), ","); got != "save-off,save-all flush,save-on" {
		t.Fatalf("RCON = %s", got)
	}
	if fin := e.col.final("job-1"); fin == nil || fin.Status != jobOK || fin.Progress != 100 || fin.Type != jobs.TypeBackup {
		t.Fatalf("final = %+v", fin)
	}
	cr := e.col.created()
	if len(cr) != 1 || cr[0].Trigger != mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_MANUAL || cr[0].ServerId != "a" || cr[0].Sha256 == "" {
		t.Fatalf("BackupCreated = %+v", cr)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(e.backupsDir(), cr[0].Filename)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.a.rec.Held("a") {
		t.Fatal("the hold must be released when it ends")
	}
}

func TestBackupNowDuplicateJobRunsOnce(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if r := e.handle(11, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("the duplicate also gets an Ack: %+v", r)
	}
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if n := strings.Count(strings.Join(e.rc.list(), ","), "save-off"); n != 1 || len(e.col.created()) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the duplicate must not repeat the backup: save-off=%d created=%d", n, len(e.col.created()))
	}
	oks := 0
	for _, env := range e.col.all() {
		if p := env.GetJobProgress(); p != nil && p.JobId == "job-1" && p.Status == jobOK {
			oks++
		}
	}
	if oks != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("the duplicate must resend the saved result (OK x2), got %d", oks)
	}
}

func TestApplySpecDuringBackupWaitsUntilItEnds(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.rc.block, e.rc.reached = make(chan struct{}), make(chan struct{})
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	waitClosed(t, e.rc.reached) // backup in progress, with save-off sent
	spec2 := strings.Replace(specJSON, `"memory":"2G"`, `"memory":"3G"`, 1)
	r := e.handle(20, &mcsmv1.ApplySpec{Server: &mcsmv1.ServerSpec{ServerId: "a", Slug: "alpha", Version: 2, DesiredState: mcsmv1.DesiredState_DESIRED_STATE_RUNNING, SpecJson: []byte(spec2)}})
	if r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("ApplySpec during the backup = %+v", r)
	}
	if fakeCalls(e.fake, "Remove") != 0 || fakeCalls(e.fake, "Create") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the ApplySpec must not recreate the container during the backup: %v", e.fake.Calls)
	}
	if v, _ := e.st.Applied("a"); v != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("applied = %d during the backup", v)
	}
	close(e.rc.block)
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if fin := e.col.final("job-1"); fin == nil || fin.Status != jobOK {
		t.Fatalf("final = %+v", fin)
	}
	if fakeCalls(e.fake, "Remove") != 1 || fakeCalls(e.fake, "Create") != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("when the backup ends the spec is applied: %v", e.fake.Calls)
	}
	if v, _ := e.st.Applied("a"); v != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("applied = %d after the backup", v)
	}
}

func TestBackupNowWhileBusy(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.rc.block, e.rc.reached = make(chan struct{}), make(chan struct{})
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	waitClosed(t, e.rc.reached)
	r := e.handle(11, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-2"})
	if r.GetError() == nil || r.GetError().Code != "busy" {
		t.Fatalf("resp = %+v", r)
	}
	if fin := e.col.final("job-2"); fin == nil || fin.Status != jobFailed {
		t.Fatalf("job-2 = %+v", fin)
	}
	close(e.rc.block)
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
}

func TestBackupNowValidation(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	if r := e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "otro", JobId: "j"}); r.GetError().GetCode() != "unknown_server" {
		t.Fatalf("foreign server = %+v", r)
	}
	if r := e.handle(11, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "j", Trigger: mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_RESTORE}); r.GetError().GetCode() != "invalid_request" {
		t.Fatalf("trigger not allowed = %+v", r)
	}
	if r := e.handle(12, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a"}); r.GetError() == nil {
		t.Fatal("without job_id must fail")
	}
}

func TestRestoreKeepsPreviousDataAndRestarts(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	name := e.col.created()[0].Filename
	e.writeLevel(t, "LEVEL-2")
	e.clk.Advance(time.Hour)
	stops := fakeCalls(e.fake, "Stop")
	if r := e.handle(20, &mcsmv1.BackupRestore{ServerId: "a", Filename: name, JobId: "job-r"}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("resp = %+v", r)
	}
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if fin := e.col.final("job-r"); fin == nil || fin.Status != jobOK || fin.Type != jobs.TypeRestore {
		t.Fatalf("final = %+v", fin)
	}
	if e.readLevel(t, e.dataDir()) != "LEVEL-1" {
		t.Fatal("the backup was not restored")
	}
	cr := e.col.created()
	pre := cr[len(cr)-1]
	if pre.Trigger != mcsmv1.BackupTrigger_BACKUP_TRIGGER_PRE_RESTORE || !strings.HasPrefix(pre.Filename, "data.pre-restore-") {
		t.Fatalf("pre-restore = %+v", pre)
	}
	if e.readLevel(t, filepath.Join(e.a.cfg.DataRoot, "alpha", pre.Filename)) != "LEVEL-2" {
		t.Fatal("the previous data must be kept")
	}
	if fakeCalls(e.fake, "Stop") <= stops {
		t.Fatal("must stop the server before restoring")
	}
	cid := e.a.rec.ContainerID(context.Background(), "a")
	if ins, _ := e.fake.Inspect(context.Background(), cid); !ins.Running {
		t.Fatal("on finishing it starts again (desired state running)")
	}
}

func TestRestoreRejectsBadName(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	r := e.handle(20, &mcsmv1.BackupRestore{ServerId: "a", Filename: "../../etc/passwd", JobId: "job-r"})
	if r.GetError() == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid name must be rejected")
	}
	if e.col.final("job-r") != nil || e.a.rec.Held("a") {
		t.Fatal("must not create a job or hold the server")
	}
}

func TestBackupListSendsInventory(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if r := e.handle(30, &mcsmv1.BackupList{}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("resp = %+v", r)
	}
	var inv *mcsmv1.BackupInventory
	for _, env := range e.col.all() {
		if m := env.GetBackupInventory(); m != nil {
			inv = m
		}
	}
	if inv == nil || len(inv.ServerIds) != 1 || inv.ServerIds[0] != "a" || len(inv.Backups) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("inventory = %+v", inv)
	}
	if len(inv.Jobs) != 1 || inv.Jobs[0].JobId != "job-1" || inv.Jobs[0].Status != jobOK {
		t.Fatalf("jobs = %+v", inv.Jobs)
	}
	if r := e.handle(31, &mcsmv1.BackupList{ServerId: "otro"}); r.GetError().GetCode() != "unknown_server" {
		t.Fatalf("foreign server = %+v", r)
	}
}

func TestBackupDelete(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	e.handle(10, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "job-1"})
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	name := e.col.created()[0].Filename
	for i, bad := range []string{"../data", "data", "alpha"} {
		if r := e.handle(uint64(40+i), &mcsmv1.BackupDelete{ServerId: "a", Filename: bad}); r.GetError() == nil {
			t.Errorf("deleting %q must fail", bad)
		}
	}
	if r := e.handle(50, &mcsmv1.BackupDelete{ServerId: "a", Filename: name}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("resp = %+v", r)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(e.backupsDir(), name)); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("the backup must be deleted")
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(e.dataDir()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal("the data is not touched")
	}
}

func TestScheduledBackupsArePrunedByKeepCount(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, backupsSpecJSON) // * * * * *, keepCount 2, no onlyIfPlayersSince
	e.a.bk.refreshSchedules()
	if ids := e.a.bk.sched.IDs(); len(ids) != 1 || ids[0] != "a" {
		t.Fatalf("scheduled = %v", ids)
	}
	for i := 0; i < 3; i++ {
		e.a.bk.onSchedule("a")
		e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
		e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	}
	metas, _ := backup.List(e.backupsDir(), "alpha")
	if len(metas) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("keepCount=2 → %d backups", len(metas))
	}
	for _, m := range metas {
		if m.Trigger != backup.TriggerScheduled {
			t.Fatalf("trigger = %s", m.Trigger)
		}
	}
	if d := e.col.deleted(); len(d) != 1 || d[0].Reason != "prune" || d[0].ServerId != "a" {
		t.Fatalf("BackupDeleted = %+v", d)
	}
	// a manual backup does not count for keepCount nor is it pruned
	e.handle(60, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "manual-1"})
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	e.a.bk.onSchedule("a")
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if metas, _ = backup.List(e.backupsDir(), "alpha"); len(metas) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("2 scheduled + 1 manual, there are %d", len(metas))
	}
}

func TestScheduledOnlyIfPlayersSince(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, strings.Replace(backupsSpecJSON, `"onlyIfPlayersSince":false`, `"onlyIfPlayersSince":true`, 1))
	count := func() int { m, _ := backup.List(e.backupsDir(), "alpha"); return len(m) }
	e.a.bk.onSchedule("a")
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if count() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("the first backup is always made")
	}
	e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	e.a.bk.onSchedule("a")
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if count() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("without players since the last backup it is skipped")
	}
	e.a.bk.players.Observe("a", 2)
	e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	e.a.bk.onSchedule("a")
	e.a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if count() != 2 {
		t.Fatal("with players since the last backup it is made")
	}
}

func TestStartFailsJobsLeftRunning(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, specJSON)
	if err := e.st.PutJob(&mcsmv1.JobRecord{JobId: "hung", ServerId: "a", Type: jobs.TypeBackup, Status: mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING, StartedUnixMs: 1}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	e.a.bk.start(ctx)
	rec, _ := e.st.GetJob("hung")
	if rec.Status != jobFailed || !strings.Contains(rec.Message, "interrupted") {
		t.Fatalf("rec = %+v", rec)
	}
}
```
