`panel/apps/web/src/lib/backups.ts`:
```ts
import { backupsSchema, type BackupsConfig } from "@mcsm/shared";

export const CRON_PRESETS = [
  { value: "daily", label: "Every day at 4:00", cron: "0 4 * * *" },
  { value: "6h", label: "Every 6 hours", cron: "0 */6 * * *" },
  { value: "hourly", label: "Every hour", cron: "0 * * * *" },
  { value: "weekly", label: "Every Sunday at 4:00", cron: "0 4 * * 0" },
  { value: "custom", label: "Custom", cron: "" },
] as const;

/** Preset matching an expression ("custom" if none). */
export function presetFor(cron: string): string {
  return CRON_PRESETS.find((p) => p.cron !== "" && p.cron === cron.trim())?.value ?? "custom";
}

/** Schedule form state (numbers as text, excludes one per line). */
export interface ScheduleForm {
  enabled: boolean; cron: string; keepDays: string; keepCount: string; excludes: string; onlyIfPlayersSince: boolean;
}

export function scheduleToForm(b: BackupsConfig | undefined): ScheduleForm {
  const d = b ?? backupsSchema.parse({});
  return {
    enabled: d.enabled, cron: d.cron, keepDays: String(d.keepDays), keepCount: String(d.keepCount),
    excludes: d.excludes.join("\n"), onlyIfPlayersSince: d.onlyIfPlayersSince,
  };
}

/** Validates with the same schema as the panel and the agent. */
export function formToSchedule(f: ScheduleForm): { ok: true; value: BackupsConfig } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const num = (s: string) => (s.trim() === "" ? 0 : Number(s.trim()));
  const keepDays = num(f.keepDays);
  const keepCount = num(f.keepCount);
  if (!Number.isInteger(keepDays) || !Number.isInteger(keepCount)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "Days and number of backups must be integers" };
  }
  const r = backupsSchema.safeParse({
    enabled: f.enabled, cron: f.cron.trim(), keepDays, keepCount, onlyIfPlayersSince: f.onlyIfPlayersSince,
    excludes: f.excludes.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== ""),
  });
  if (!r.success) return { ok: false, error: r.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid schedule" };
  return { ok: true, value: r.data };
}
```

```tsx
import type { JobDto } from "@/lib/api";
import { jobTypeLabel } from "@/lib/jobs";

export function ProgressBar({ value }: { value: number }) {
  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value));
  return (
    <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-zinc-800" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={v}>
      <div className="h-full bg-emerald-500 transition-[width]" style={{ width: `${v}%` }} />
    </div>
  );
}

/** Card with the job in progress (backup or restore). */
export function JobProgress({ job }: { job: JobDto }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-md border border-zinc-800 p-3 text-sm" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between gap-2">
        <span>{jobTypeLabel(job.type)} in progress: {job.message || "…"}</span>
        <span className="tabular-nums text-zinc-400">{job.progress}%</span>
      </div>
      <ProgressBar value={job.progress} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`panel/apps/web/src/components/backup-schedule-form.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { BackupsConfig } from "@mcsm/shared";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { api, ApiError, type ServerDto } from "@/lib/api";
import { CRON_PRESETS, formToSchedule, presetFor, scheduleToForm, type ScheduleForm } from "@/lib/backups";

/** Backup schedule and retention: PATCH of `spec.backups` (not part of the configuration form). */
export function BackupScheduleForm({ server }: { server: ServerDto }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [f, setF] = useState<ScheduleForm>(() => scheduleToForm(server.spec.backups));
  const [localErr, setLocalErr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const set = <K extends keyof ScheduleForm>(k: K, v: ScheduleForm[K]) => setF((o) => ({ ...o, [k]: v }));
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (cfg: BackupsConfig) => api.patch<ServerDto>(`/api/servers/${server.id}/backups/schedule`, cfg),
    onSuccess: (dto) => { qc.setQueryData(["server", server.id], dto); void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["servers"] }); },
  });
  const submit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLocalErr(null);
    const r = formToSchedule(f);
    if (!r.ok) return setLocalErr(r.error);
    save.mutate(r.value);
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex max-w-3xl flex-col gap-4">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <input type="checkbox" className="size-4 accent-emerald-500" checked={f.enabled} onChange={(e) => set("enabled", e.target.checked)} />
        Scheduled backups
      </label>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div>
          <Label>Frequency</Label>
          <Select value={presetFor(f.cron)} onValueChange={(v) => { const p = CRON_PRESETS.find((x) => x.value === v); if (p?.cron) set("cron", p.cron); }}>
            <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>{CRON_PRESETS.map((p) => <SelectItem key={p.value} value={p.value}>{p.label}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <Label htmlFor="backup-cron">Cron expression (node time)</Label>
          <Input id="backup-cron" value={f.cron} onChange={(e) => set("cron", e.target.value)} className="font-mono" placeholder="0 4 * * *" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <Label htmlFor="backup-days">Keep days (0 = no limit)</Label>
          <Input id="backup-days" inputMode="numeric" value={f.keepDays} onChange={(e) => set("keepDays", e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <Label htmlFor="backup-count">Keep backups (0 = no limit)</Label>
          <Input id="backup-count" inputMode="numeric" value={f.keepCount} onChange={(e) => set("keepCount", e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
        Retention only deletes scheduled backups (never the most recent one). Manual backups and data from before a restore are kept until you delete them.
      </p>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <input type="checkbox" className="size-4 accent-emerald-500" checked={f.onlyIfPlayersSince} onChange={(e) => set("onlyIfPlayersSince", e.target.checked)} />
        Only if someone has played since the last backup
      </label>
      <div>
        <Label htmlFor="backup-excludes">Exclude (one pattern per line)</Label>
        <Textarea id="backup-excludes" rows={4} className="font-mono text-xs" value={f.excludes} onChange={(e) => set("excludes", e.target.value)} />
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">
          A name without “/” is excluded at any depth (logs, *.tmp); with “/”, the exact path (world/session.lock). Add *.jar to stop copying the .jar files the image downloads again (careful: this also covers the ones you uploaded by hand).
        </p>
      </div>
      {(localErr || save.error) && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-400">{localErr ?? (save.error as ApiError).message}</p>}
      {save.isSuccess && <p className="text-sm text-emerald-400">Schedule saved (spec v{save.data.specVersion}).</p>}
      <div><Button type="submit" disabled={save.isPending}>Save schedule</Button></div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { DatabaseBackup, Download, History, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import { BackupScheduleForm } from "@/components/backup-schedule-form";
import { JobProgress } from "@/components/job-progress";
import { useBackups } from "@/hooks/use-backups";
import { useJobs, useJobsLive } from "@/hooks/use-jobs";
import { api, ApiError, type BackupDto, type JobDto, type ServerDto } from "@/lib/api";
import { formatBytes } from "@/lib/format";
import { backupsKey, isActive, TRIGGER_LABEL } from "@/lib/jobs";

const when = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleString("es-ES");

export function BackupsTab({ server, nodeOnline }: { server: ServerDto; nodeOnline: boolean | undefined }) {
  useJobsLive();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const backups = useBackups(server.id);
  const jobs = useJobs({ serverId: server.id });
  const active = jobs.data?.find((j) => isActive(j) && (j.type === "backup" || j.type === "restore"));
  const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState<BackupDto | null>(null);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<BackupDto | null>(null);
  const refreshJobs = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["jobs"] });

  const createNow = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api.post<JobDto>(`/api/servers/${server.id}/backups`), onSuccess: refreshJobs });
  const restore = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (b: BackupDto) => api.post<JobDto>(`/api/servers/${server.id}/backups/${b.id}/restore`),
    onSuccess: () => { setRestoring(null); void refreshJobs(); },
  });
  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (b: BackupDto) => api.del<void>(`/api/servers/${server.id}/backups/${b.id}`),
    onSuccess: () => { setDeleting(null); void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: backupsKey(server.id) }); },
  });
  const offline = nodeOnline === false;
  const busy = offline || Boolean(active);
  const error = createNow.error ?? restore.error ?? remove.error;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <section className="flex flex-col gap-3" aria-label="Backups">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Backups</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">On the node's disk, in {server.slug}/backups.</p>
          </div>
          <Button onClick={() => createNow.mutate()} disabled={busy || createNow.isPending} title={offline ? "The node is offline" : active ? "A backup or restore is in progress" : undefined}>
            <DatabaseBackup className="size-4" /> Create backup now
          </Button>
        </div>
        {active && <JobProgress job={active} />}
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-400">{(error as ApiError).message}</p>}
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow><TableHead>Date</TableHead><TableHead>Type</TableHead><TableHead>Size</TableHead><TableHead>Spec</TableHead><TableHead><span className="sr-only">Actions</span></TableHead></TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {backups.isPending && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={5} className="text-center text-zinc-400">Loading backups…</TableCell></TableRow>}
            {backups.isError && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={5} className="text-center text-red-400">Could not load the backups: {backups.error.message}</TableCell></TableRow>}
            {backups.data?.length === 0 && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={5} className="text-center text-zinc-400">No backups yet. Create one now or schedule automatic backups below.</TableCell></TableRow>}
            {backups.data?.map((b) => (
              <TableRow key={b.id} data-testid={`backup-row-${b.filename}`}>
                <TableCell><div>{when(b.startedAt)}</div><div className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-500">{b.filename}</div></TableCell>
                <TableCell><Badge variant={b.kind === "directory" ? "outline" : "secondary"}>{TRIGGER_LABEL[b.trigger]}</Badge></TableCell>
                <TableCell>{b.sizeBytes ? formatBytes(b.sizeBytes) : "—"}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>{b.specVersion ? `v${b.specVersion}` : "—"}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>
                  <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">
                    {b.kind === "archive" && (
                      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setRestoring(b)}><History className="size-4" /> Restore</Button>
                    )}
                    {b.kind === "archive" && (
                      <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled title="Download will be available with the file manager"><Download className="size-4" /><span className="sr-only">Download</span></Button>
                    )}
                    <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled={offline || remove.isPending} onClick={() => setDeleting(b)}><Trash2 className="size-4" /><span className="sr-only">Delete</span></Button>
                  </div>
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ))}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-3" aria-label="Schedule">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Schedule</h2>
        <BackupScheduleForm key={server.specVersion} server={server} />
      </section>

      <Dialog open={restoring !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setRestoring(null); }}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Restore this backup?</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              “{server.name}” will be stopped, the current data will be set aside in a data.pre-restore-… directory (it is not deleted) and the backup from {restoring ? when(restoring.startedAt) : ""} will be extracted. Afterwards the server will start again if it was running.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRestoring(null)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button variant="destructive" disabled={restore.isPending} onClick={() => restoring && restore.mutate(restoring)}>Restore</Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <Dialog open={deleting !== null} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDeleting(null); }}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>{deleting?.kind === "directory" ? "Delete the data from before the restore?" : "Delete this backup?"}</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              {deleting?.kind === "directory"
                ? `The directory ${deleting.filename} with the world from before the restore will be deleted from the node. This cannot be undone.`
                : `The file ${deleting?.filename ?? ""} will be deleted from the node. This cannot be undone.`}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleting(null)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button variant="destructive" disabled={remove.isPending} onClick={() => deleting && remove.mutate(deleting)}>Delete</Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 15: Web — History tab and Jobs page

**Agent:** web-ui-dev.

**Files:**
- Create: `panel/apps/web/src/components/job-list.tsx`, `panel/apps/web/src/routes/jobs.tsx`
- Modify: `panel/apps/web/src/router.tsx` (`plan-4` anchors), `panel/apps/web/src/routes/server.tsx` (`plan-4` anchors), `panel/apps/web/src/components/layout.tsx` (menu entry; outside the anchor)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 13 and 14 (`useJobs`, `useJob`, `useJobsLive`, `ProgressBar`, labels), `useServers` (Plan 1).
- Produces: `<JobList filter showServer? />` (live table; clicking a row expands its log, which grows live and is reloaded in full when the job finishes); `JobsPage` with filter All/In progress/Succeeded/Failed; route `/jobs` (`jobsRoute`); tab "History" (the server's jobs).

- [ ] **Step 1: Components**

`panel/apps/web/src/components/job-list.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Fragment, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import { ProgressBar } from "@/components/job-progress";
import { useJob, useJobs, useJobsLive } from "@/hooks/use-jobs";
import { useServers } from "@/hooks/use-servers";
import type { JobState } from "@/lib/api";
import { isActive, JOB_STATUS_LABEL, jobTriggerLabel, jobTypeLabel, type JobsFilter } from "@/lib/jobs";

const VARIANT: Record<JobState, "default" | "secondary" | "destructive" | "outline"> = {
  queued: "outline", running: "secondary", ok: "default", failed: "destructive", cancelled: "outline",
};

/** Live jobs table; clicking a row expands its log. */
export function JobList({ filter, showServer = true }: { filter: JobsFilter; showServer?: boolean }) {
  useJobsLive();
  const jobs = useJobs(filter);
  const servers = useServers();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const serverName = (id: string) => servers.data?.find((s) => s.id === id)?.name ?? "(deleted)";
  const cols = showServer ? 6 : 5;
  return (
    <Table>
      <TableHeader>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHead className="w-8"><span className="sr-only">Details</span></TableHead>
          <TableHead>Type</TableHead>
          {showServer && <TableHead>Server</TableHead>}
          <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
          <TableHead>Progress</TableHead>
          <TableHead>Start</TableHead>
        </TableRow>
      </TableHeader>
      <TableBody>
        {jobs.isPending && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={cols} className="text-center text-zinc-400">Loading…</TableCell></TableRow>}
        {jobs.isError && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={cols} className="text-center text-red-400">Could not load the jobs: {jobs.error.message}</TableCell></TableRow>}
        {jobs.data?.length === 0 && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={cols} className="text-center text-zinc-400">No jobs.</TableCell></TableRow>}
        {jobs.data?.map((j) => {
          const trigger = jobTriggerLabel(j);
          return (
            <Fragment key={j.id}>
              <TableRow className="cursor-pointer" onClick={() => setOpen(open === j.id ? null : j.id)} data-testid={`job-row-${j.id}`} aria-expanded={open === j.id}>
                <TableCell>{open === j.id ? <ChevronDown className="size-4" /> : <ChevronRight className="size-4" />}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>{jobTypeLabel(j.type)}{trigger && <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">({trigger.toLowerCase()})</span>}</TableCell>
                {showServer && (
                  <TableCell>
                    {j.serverId
                      ? <Link to="/servers/$serverId" params={{ serverId: j.serverId }} className="hover:underline" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>{serverName(j.serverId)}</Link>
                      : <span className="text-zinc-500">—</span>}
                  </TableCell>
                )}
                <TableCell>
                  <Badge variant={VARIANT[j.status]}>{JOB_STATUS_LABEL[j.status]}</Badge>
                  {j.message && <div className="mt-1 max-w-md truncate text-xs text-zinc-400" title={j.message}>{j.message}</div>}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="w-40">{isActive(j) ? <ProgressBar value={j.progress} /> : <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{j.progress}%</span>}</TableCell>
                <TableCell className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                  {new Date(j.createdAt).toLocaleString("es-ES")}
                  {j.finishedAt && <div>finished {new Date(j.finishedAt).toLocaleTimeString("es-ES")}</div>}
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
              {open === j.id && <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={cols}><JobLog id={j.id} /></TableCell></TableRow>}
            </Fragment>
          );
        })}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
}

function JobLog({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const q = useJob(id);
  if (q.isPending) return <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">Loading log…</p>;
  if (q.isError) return <p className="text-xs text-red-400">{q.error.message}</p>;
  return <pre className="max-h-80 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-all rounded bg-black p-3 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-300">{q.data.log || "(no log)"}</pre>;
}
```

`panel/apps/web/src/routes/jobs.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { JobList } from "@/components/job-list";
import type { JobState } from "@/lib/api";

const FILTERS: { value: JobState | null; label: string }[] = [
  { value: null, label: "All" }, { value: "running", label: "In progress" }, { value: "ok", label: "Succeeded" }, { value: "failed", label: "Failed" },
];

export function JobsPage() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<JobState | null>(null);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Jobs</h1>
        <div className="flex gap-1" role="group" aria-label="Filter by status">
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <Button key={f.label} size="sm" variant={status === f.value ? "secondary" : "ghost"} aria-pressed={status === f.value} onClick={() => setStatus(f.value)}>{f.label}</Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </header>
      <JobList filter={status ? { status } : {}} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: Integration with real Docker and end-to-end verification

**Agent:** infra-dev (if the integration test fails without an obvious cause, `debugger`).

**Files:**
- Create: `agent/internal/app/backup_integration_test.go`
- Modify (only if there are deviations): this plan, as a footnote to the task

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole plan; `docker.NewMoby` (Plan 1); `deploy/docker-compose.dev.yml` (Plan 1).
- Produces: `TestIntegrationBackupRestore` (tag `integration`): real itzg VANILLA server (`mcsm-test-backup`, network `mcsm-test-backup-net`), hot backup, check that autosave is left on (`save-on` → "Saving is already turned on"), restore that sets the data aside and returns to `healthy`, cleanup. Uses `imageTag: "latest"` to take advantage of the local image; it needs uid 1000 (or root) because the image writes `/data` with that uid. The test runs on the host, outside the Docker network, so RCON goes through `docker exec <container> rcon-cli`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Integration test**

`agent/internal/app/backup_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/backup"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/store"
)

// Test against real Docker (spec §11): itzg VANILLA server, hot backup with save-off/save-on,
// restore and cleanup. It only creates mcsm-test-* resources. The first run takes several
// minutes (itzg image and server download).
func TestIntegrationBackupRestore(t *testing.T) {
	if uid := os.Getuid(); uid != 0 && uid != 1000 {
		t.Skip("the itzg image writes /data with uid 1000: run the test as that user or as root")
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 15*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	eng, err := docker.NewMoby()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	const netName, serverID, slug = "mcsm-test-backup-net", "int-backup", "test-backup" // container mcsm-test-backup
	if err := eng.EnsureNetwork(ctx, netName); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	st, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "agent.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { st.Close() }) // Cleanup is LIFO: it is closed after the server is deleted
	a := New(Config{DataRoot: t.TempDir(), Network: netName, Version: "integration"}, st, eng, slog.Default())
	col := &collector{}
	a.send = col.send
	// The test runs on the host, outside the Docker network: RCON via the itzg image's rcon-cli.
	a.rcon = func(ctx context.Context, host string, _ uint16, _ string, cmd string) (string, error) {
		out, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "docker", append([]string{"exec", host, "rcon-cli"}, strings.Fields(cmd)...)...).CombinedOutput()
		if err != nil {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("%v: %s", err, out)
		}
		return string(out), nil
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
		defer cancel()
		if err := a.rec.Remove(cctx, serverID, false); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("cleanup: %v (remove the mcsm-test-backup container by hand)", err)
		}
		_ = exec.Command("docker", "network", "rm", netName).Run()
	})

	specJS := fmt.Sprintf(`{"imageTag":"latest","type":"VANILLA","version":"1.21.4","memory":"1G","ports":{"game":%d},"restartPolicy":"no","rconPassword":"integration-pass","env":{"ONLINE_MODE":"FALSE"}}`, freePort(t))
	if err := a.rec.Apply(ctx, &mcsmv1.ServerSpec{ServerId: serverID, Slug: slug, Version: 1, DesiredState: mcsmv1.DesiredState_DESIRED_STATE_RUNNING, SpecJson: []byte(specJS)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	waitHealthy(ctx, t, a, serverID)

	// 1. Hot backup.
	a.Handle(ctx, &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: 1, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupNow{BackupNow: &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: serverID, JobId: "int-job-1"}}}, col.send)
	a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if fin := col.final("int-job-1"); fin == nil || fin.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK {
		t.Fatalf("backup = %+v", fin)
	}
	out, err := a.rcon(ctx, "mcsm-"+slug, 0, "", "save-on")
	if err != nil || !strings.Contains(out, "already") {
		t.Fatalf("after the backup autosave must be on (save-on → %q, %v)", out, err)
	}
	created := col.created()
	name := created[0].Filename
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(backup.Dir(a.cfg.DataRoot, slug), name))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	probe := t.TempDir()
	if err := backup.ExtractArchive(ctx, bytes.NewReader(data), probe, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(probe, "world", "level.dat")); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the backup must contain the world: %v", err)
	}

	// 2. Restore: the current data is set aside and the server starts again.
	dataDir := filepath.Join(a.cfg.DataRoot, slug, "data")
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dataDir, "marca-posterior.txt"), []byte("x"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a.Handle(ctx, &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: 2, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_BackupRestore{BackupRestore: &mcsmv1.BackupRestore{ServerId: serverID, Filename: name, JobId: "int-job-2"}}}, col.send)
	a.bk.wg.Wait()
	if fin := col.final("int-job-2"); fin == nil || fin.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_OK {
		t.Fatalf("restore = %+v", fin)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dataDir, "marca-posterior.txt")); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("after the restore the post-backup marker must not be there")
	}
	pres, _ := backup.ListPreRestore(dataDir)
	if len(pres) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pre-restore = %+v", pres)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(a.cfg.DataRoot, slug, pres[0].Filename, "marca-posterior.txt")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal("the previous data must be kept in the pre-restore directory")
	}
	waitHealthy(ctx, t, a, serverID)
}

func freePort(t *testing.T) int {
	t.Helper()
	l, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer l.Close()
	return l.Addr().(*net.TCPAddr).Port
}

// waitHealthy polls the itzg image healthcheck until the server responds.
func waitHealthy(ctx context.Context, t *testing.T, a *App, serverID string) {
	t.Helper()
	for {
		if obs := a.rec.Observe(ctx, serverID); obs.Health == "healthy" {
			return
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			t.Fatal("the server did not become healthy in time")
		case <-time.After(5 * time.Second): // polling an external resource: waiting is fine here
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: End-to-end acceptance (manual, real Docker)**

Setup (as in Plan 1): `docker network create mcsm` if it does not exist; `pnpm dev:api` and `pnpm dev:web`; node enrollment and `ENROLL_TOKEN=<token> DATA_ROOT=$PWD/deploy/data docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.dev.yml up -d --build`. Create the server **"test-backups"** (slug `test-backups` → container `mcsm-test-backups`), VANILLA, LATEST, 2G, started; wait for "Running".

1. **Manual backup, autosave left enabled** (acceptance 1). Backups tab → "Create backup now": the progress bar appears and then the "Manual" row with its size; in `/jobs` the job ends "Succeeded" and its log shows `save-off`, `save-all flush`, `save-on: autosave re-enabled`. Check:
   ```bash
   docker logs mcsm-test-backups 2>&1 | grep -i "automatic saving"
   ls deploy/data/test-backups/backups/
   ```
   Expected: the last line of `grep` is "Automatic saving is now enabled" (after "…disabled"); in the Console, `save-on` replies "Saving is already turned on"; there is a `.tar.zst` and its `.json`.
2. **Restore leaves the previous world renamed** (acceptance 2). `touch deploy/data/test-backups/data/marca.txt` (as uid 1000 or with `sudo`), and on the backup's row → "Restore" → confirm. The server goes to "Stopped" and back to "Running"; the "Restore" job ends "Succeeded"; the "Before restore" row appears with only "Delete".
   ```bash
   ls deploy/data/test-backups/
   ls deploy/data/test-backups/data/marca.txt deploy/data/test-backups/data.pre-restore-*/marca.txt
   ```
   Expected: `data` and `data.pre-restore-<ts>` exist; `marca.txt` is only in the pre-restore.
3. **Scheduled `* * * * *` respects `keepCount`** (acceptance 3). Schedule: "Scheduled backups" on, expression `* * * * *`, keep backups `3`, days `0`, without "Only if someone has played" → "Save schedule". The container is **not** recreated (`docker ps` keeps its uptime) and the spec version goes up. Wait 5 minutes:
   ```bash
   ls -1 deploy/data/test-backups/backups/*.tar.zst
   ```
   Expected: one new file per minute; exactly 3 scheduled ones remain (plus the manual backup, which is not pruned); `/jobs` shows a "Backup (scheduled)" job every minute and the Backups tab list matches the disk.
4. **Panel restarted.** Stop `pnpm dev:api` for 2 minutes with the schedule active and start it again: History shows the scheduled jobs from those minutes (they arrive with the inventory) and the backups pruned in the meantime no longer appear.
5. **Busy.** While a backup (or the restore) runs, "Create backup now" and "Restore" are disabled and hovering explains "A backup or restore is in progress"; in Configuration, "Save and apply" during the backup leaves the server "applying vN…" and the container is recreated only when the backup finishes. (The `busy` rejection of a second simultaneous order is covered by the tests in Tasks 9 and 12.)
6. **Cleanup.** Turn off the schedule, delete the server with the delete-data option and `docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.dev.yml down`. Check with `docker ps -a --filter name=mcsm-test` that nothing is left.

Record any deviation as a footnote to this task before closing the plan.

> **Verification note (wave A, real Docker):**
> - `backup_integration_test.go`: the code in the brief did not receive the job notices because the notice queue (`a.bk.out`) only runs with `Run`. The test starts it (`a.bk.loop(... out.run ...)`), uses a `newCollector()` and a `settle` (wait on `a.bk.wg` + a mark in the queue) instead of `a.bk.wg.Wait()`. With that it passes (≈55 s).
> - The E2E was run with a PAPER 1.21.4 server (not VANILLA), the panel as container `mcsm-test-panel` on the `mcsm` network (the firewall drops container → host) and the agent with `PANEL_URL=http://mcsm-test-panel:8080`. The visual steps (progress bar, disabled buttons with their tooltip, "Before restore" row) are left to a person: the automated browser does not open `localhost:8080`.
> - Final behavior also checked: writing files during a restore → 409 `busy`; restore leaves `data.pre-restore-<ts>`; `docker stop` of the agent mid-backup re-enables `save-on`, the job ends FAILED "interrupted" and no `.partial` files remain. Report: `.superpowers/sdd/wave-a-e2e-report.md`.

## Closing the plan

On finishing Task 16 the system meets step 6 of the spec's build order (§13) except for backup download, which arrives with the Plan 5 transfers in wave B (see “Coordination”). Before merging: `test-runner` with the full verification, `final-reviewer` on the branch and `security-auditor` (the plan touches file paths on the node: file extraction, directory deletion and names coming from the panel). Then `superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch` and the merge of wave A according to `COORDINATION.md`.
